Kere: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                          | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) fun ipo ilera ẹrí ipamọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                           | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Ṣeto si `1` tabi `true` lati mu idanwo igbakọọkan lẹhin ti awọn asopọ olupese. Awọn olupese wiwa (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS ni `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, fun apẹẹrẹ `tavily-search`) nigbagbogbo ni a yọkuro kuro ninu — gbigba “ifọwọsi” wọn jẹ ibeere ti o ni idiyele gidi, nitorinaa wọn kii ṣe ayẹwo ni ilera rara lori aago (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                               | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Ṣeto si `1` lati gba olupe ti o ni idaniloju lati beere `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, eyiti o ṣe ayẹwo oju-aye ti o pari ni ẹẹkan fun TTL. Awọn olupe alailorukọ ko ṣe okunfa iwadii naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                            | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Iduro ẹnu-ọna (ms) fun gbigbasilẹ iṣẹlẹ-loop iṣẹlẹ: didi gun ju eyi ṣe igbasilẹ laini `event loop stalled` kan ni ibẹrẹ pẹlu iranti ati awọn iṣiro inu-ofurufu, o si fa `eventLoopStall` counter/maximum sori `/api/monitoring/health`. Ṣeto si `0` lati mu; iye ti a ko le ka tabi odi ṣubu pada si `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Di adirẹsi fun Next.js dev/start olupin. Yiyipada `0.0.0.0` aiyipada nigbati o ba ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                       | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adirẹsi dipọ ti olusare Playwright nlo nigbati o n ṣe ifilọlẹ Next.js. Aiyipada si `127.0.0.1` fun awọn idanwo hermetic.** Maṣe lo fun `omniroute serve`** — lo `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` dipo (POSIX shells auto-ṣeto `HOSTNAME` si orukọ ẹrọ; `.env` ko le yiju rẹ kuro).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                         | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Di adirẹsi fun `omniroute serve`. Yago fun ikọlu pẹlu oniyipada `HOSTNAME` ikarahun POSIX (ṣeto nigbagbogbo si orukọ ẹrọ nipasẹ bash/zsh). Ṣubu pada si `0.0.0.0` nigbati a ko ṣeto. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Awọn ọna ibudo

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Aabo & Ijeri

| Ayípadà                                     | Aiyipada                                                                        | Faili orisun                                             | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                           | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Iyọ ni idapo pẹlu awọn idamọ ohun elo fun ika ika ẹrọ. Yi fun-imuṣiṣẹ fun ipinya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(yọ = laileto fun fifi sori ẹrọ iyọ duro ni `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | iyọ HMAC fun jijade aami auth CLI agbegbe. Ṣiṣeto iye yii n yi gbogbo awọn ami CLI sori ẹrọ ati nigbagbogbo gba pataki lori iyọ ti o duro. Wo `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                         | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Ṣeto asia `Secure` sori awọn kuki igba.** Gbọdọ jẹ `true`** nigbati o nṣiṣẹ lẹhin HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                         | API middleware                                           | Nigbati `true`, gbogbo awọn ibeere aṣoju `/v1/*` gbọdọ ni bọtini API to wulo.** Asia yi ko bode `GET /v1/models`** , eyiti o tẹle ipo iduro iwọle dasibodu (`requireAuthForModels`) dipo — nitorinaa `401` lati `/v1/models` tumọ si itọkasi NOT ni aabo. Wo `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                         | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Faye gba ṣiṣafihan awọn iye bọtini API ni kikun ninu Dashboard UI. Configurable lati Awọn asia Ẹya Dasibodu; aabo ewu lori pín instances.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(sofo)_                                                                        | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | Bọtini API ti o ya sọtọ komama IDs ti o fori ibeere gedu (ibamu GDPR ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(Ailopin = aito)_                                                              | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Isunwo ibeere fun ọjọ-ojo kan ti a lo si awọn bọtini API ti ọwọn `rate_limits` wọn jẹ asan. Ti ko ṣeto tabi ofo: ko si fila kan (#2289, #11017). `0` jẹ kanna (ailopin). Odidi N jẹ ki N/day, 5N/week, 20N/month. Awọn iye aiṣedeede ti ko ṣofo ṣubu pada si ogún 1000/day, 5000/week, awọn window 20000/month.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                              | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | O pọju laaye ìbéèrè ara iwọn. Kọ awọn fifuye isanwo ti o kọja opin yii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                               | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Awọn ara ibeere gangan ni tabi loke iloro yii gba ilana atomiki-igbanilaaye gbigba iwuwo iwuwo agbegbe ṣaaju sisọ JSON (ọna BYTE, pẹlu `POST /v1/responses`). Kanna [# 10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) ni ilera-headroom ona abayo bi be-eru; si tun didi `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [# 10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ki [# 7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ko pada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                              | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Wiregbe-ona fila lile ti a fi agbara mu lodi si awọn baiti kika nigba didi didi, pẹlu awọn ibeere pẹlu sonu, invalid, tabi aiṣootọ `Content-Length`; excess gba `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _ (a ko ṣeto - ko si fila-ka ibeere) _                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **# 503-afẹfẹ:** Ibeere-ipamọ-COUNT fila bayi di asopọ nikan nigbati o ba ṣeto ni gbangba. Ti a ko ṣeto ni osi (aiyipada), gbigba iwiregbe iwuwo wuwo jẹ dipo titọ nipasẹ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — isuna BYTE ti a mu ni adaṣe ti o ni iwọn lati aja iranti gidi ti ilana naa ni** ọkan ilana** (okiti V8 kan). Meji agbekọja ~ 750k-àmi `/v1/responses` abort ~ 12 Gi òkiti (#7849) - a** iranti-isuna** Ikilọ, kii ṣe ọja lile ti o pọju ti 2. Ilana ti ilera (okiti ni isalẹ ipin ti o ta silẹ) MAY gba diẹ sii ni igbakanna gun `/v1/responses` nipasẹ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Mewa ti gun SSE ibara (40–50) jẹ òkiti + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ko "max 2". Ni afọju igbega eyi lati “lo agbalejo” tun ṣe #7849. Isodipupo** òkiti** pẹlu** N ominira `DATA_DIR` s** (# 11024); rara `replicas>1` lori faili SQLite kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(ti ari ni aladaaṣe)_                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **# 503-afẹfẹ:** yiyokuro fun isuna baiti ingest ti a mu jade laifọwọyi (25% ti orule iranti V8/cgroup tighter ti o pin nipasẹ 8x imudara igba diẹ). Ti ari ati awọn iye fojuhan dimole si 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ara ti o tobi ju isuna ti o munadoko lọ kuna lẹsẹkẹsẹ pẹlu `413 body_exceeds_budget`; àríyànjiyàn laarin awọn ara ẹni kọọkan ti o le ṣe iṣẹ si wa tun le gbiyanju `503`. 40–50 awọn alabara SSE gigun nigbakanna jẹ opo + isuna yii, kii ṣe “max 2” lile. Ka `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` ni `/api/monitoring/health` ṣaaju ṣiṣe atunṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ìpín ìtalẹ̀ òkìtì (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) fún BYTE àti STRUCTURE gbígba ìwọ̀n òwú (#10183, #10268, #10437). Ibeere iwuwo iwuwo nigbakanna ti o kọja `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` yoo ta silẹ nikan pẹlu `503` ti a tun gbiyanju nigbati okiti naa ba jẹ ALSO ni tabi loke ipin yii; lori okiti ti o ni ilera o gba wọle nipasẹ yara-ile ilera dipo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (aiyipada `1`)                             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Iwọn afikun agbara fun ọna iyara-okiti ilera (#10437) lori** mejeeji** STRUCTURE ati BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, pẹlu awọn ara ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Laisi idii yii, gbogbo ibeere okiti ti o nšišẹ ṣugbọn-ilera ti kọja gbigba gbigba laisi aja. Ni kete ti ọpọlọpọ awọn iyalo nigbakanna ni o ṣiṣẹ nipasẹ ọna okiti ilera, awọn ibeere ti o nšišẹ siwaju ṣubu nipasẹ ọna SAME bounded-wait/shed ti a lo labẹ titẹ okiti gidi. `0` mu fori naa kuro patapata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                         | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Pa awọn sọwedowo oluso URL ti o njade lo, bulọki-metadata awọsanma ti o wa pẹlu (gbogbo 169.254.0.0/16, metadata.google.internal), lori awọn ọna olupese ti o tẹle ipo ẹṣọ: afọwọsi URL, iṣawari awoṣe, olupese-node mimọ URLs (pẹlu awọn apa rerank isakoṣo latọna jijin), ati idanwo aṣoju-fallback, laarin awọn olupese miiran ti o wa nitosi ọna ti image/media wa nitosi. Awọn ibeere iwiregbe ti a firanṣẹ nipasẹ `BaseExecutor.execute()` tọju ayẹwo-metadata awọsanma wọn. Awọn olupese agbegbe n ṣiṣẹ pẹlu awọn aṣiṣe: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (aiyipada `true`) tẹlẹ gba agbegbe ati LAN URLs laaye lori afọwọsi, iṣawari, ati awọn ipa ọna olupese-node; idanwo aṣoju-fallback ati awọn ibi-afẹde webhook aladani kan ṣagbero asia yii nikan ki o si di awọn ogun local/LAN duro lakoko ti o wa ni pipa. Yiyọ kuro `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Iye kan ti o fipamọ lati toggle dasibodu gba iṣaaju lori oniyipada yii.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                          | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Gba URLs olupese laaye lori awọn adirẹsi local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, awọn sakani ikọkọ) fun ijẹrisi olupese, iṣawari awoṣe, iwiregbe, ati awọn ọna itagbangba ti olupese miiran ti o wa nitosi ipo iṣọ n ṣakoso.** aiyipada `true`** (agbegbe-akọkọ): ẹṣọ lẹhinna ṣe idinamọ awọn aaye ipari-metadata awọsanma - gbogbo 169.254.0.0/16 pẹlu awọn orukọ olupin metadata ti a mọ (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Ṣeto `false` fun ipo gbogbogbo-nikan, eyiti o ṣe idiwọ ikọkọ ati awọn agbalejo loopback paapaa. Oluṣọ ṣayẹwo orukọ olupin tabi IP gangan gẹgẹbi a ti kọ, ṣaaju wiwa DNS eyikeyi; ni ipo gbangba-nikan ohun ipari-ailewu ni afikun si ipinnu orukọ olupin ati kọ eyikeyi idahun ikọkọ. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` yi awọn sọwedowo wọnyi kuro lori awọn ọna awọn atokọ ila rẹ. Awọn olupese agbegbe ti a ṣe sinu, gẹgẹbi LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, ati Triton, fo gbogbo awọn sọwedowo agbalejo fun ijẹrisi bọtini ati iwiregbe. Iye kan ti o fipamọ lati toggle dasibodu gba iṣaaju lori oniyipada yii. (#5066) |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                         | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Jẹ ki awọn ipa ọna `/v1/audio/*` (awọn iwe afọwọkọ, ọrọ sisọ, awọn itumọ) lo oju ipade olupese ibaramu OpenAI ti a gbalejo ni ita localhost. Paa nipasẹ aiyipada - ohun afetigbọ si agbalejo latọna jijin yipada idanimọ egress ati pe o gbọdọ jẹ ipinnu oniṣẹ ti o fojuhan. Loopback/private nodes (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ti wa ni nigbagbogbo laaye ati ki o ko ni ipa. | `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                         | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Jẹ ki `POST /v1/rerank` (ati awọn ẹrọ iranti loopback rerank igbese) lo ohun OpenAI-ibaramu olupese ipade ti gbalejo ita localhost — a LAN apoti tabi Tailscale ẹlẹgbẹ nṣiṣẹ TEI, Infinity, vLLM, ati be be lo Pa nipa aiyipada — afisona si a latọna ogun ayipada egress idanimo ati ki o gbọdọ jẹ ohun fojuhan onišẹ ipinnu. Loopback apa (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) ti wa ni nigbagbogbo laaye ati ki o ko ni ipa. URL node jijin kan gbọdọ tun kọja eto imulo URL ti olupese ti njade (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(sofo)_                                                                        | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Awọn orukọ igbalejo (koma tabi aaye funfun ti o yapa) ti awọn itọju ipa ọna olupese-ipin si bi agbegbe — ni igbagbogbo orukọ iṣẹ Docker/Compose ti reranker, TEI tabi sidecar ohun lori OmniRoute ti ara Docker nẹtiwọki (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), nitorina ipade naa ko nilo apoti ti o ni koodu IP. Awọn ogun ti a ṣe akojọ ni ẹtọ fun `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` ati ayẹwo ilera agbegbe laisi titan `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` fun gbogbo oju ipade latọna jijin, ati pe wọn pe ni taara - rara nipasẹ `HTTP(S)_PROXY` tabi aṣoju asopọ asopọ. Wọn tun firanṣẹ bọtini API asopọ naa. Awọn orukọ aami ẹyọkan ati awọn orukọ `.internal`/`.local` ni a gba; FQDNs ti gbogbo eniyan, awọn ọrọ gangan IP ati awọn orukọ-metadata awọsanma jẹ aibikita pẹlu ikilọ kan. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                         | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Nigbati OIDC ba ti ṣiṣẹ, mu iwọle iwọle si ọrọ igbaniwọle jẹ ki awọn olumulo le jẹri nipasẹ OIDC Nikan Wọle Kan. Awọn inagijẹ inagijẹ `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` tun gba; Flag Ẹya Dasibodu ti bọtini kanna gba iṣaaju. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                         | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Igboro inagijẹ ti `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Lile Atokọ

```bash
# Production security minimum:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Requires HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Authenticate all proxy calls
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Never expose keys in UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # 5 MB limit
```

## 5. Input Sanitization & PII Idaabobo

OmniRoute n pese aabo Layer-meji: Ṣiṣayẹwo abẹrẹ ẹgbẹ-ibeere ati yiyọ PII apa esi.

> **Awọn idiwọn ⚠️ :** Awọn ọna iṣọṣọ wọnyi jẹ awọn iwadii heuristic_ _best-akitiyan, kii ṣe ogiriina abẹrẹ pipe tabi eto PII DLP. Wọn le ṣe agbejade awọn idaniloju eke (awọn itọsi persona/RPG ko dara) ati awọn odi eke (leetspeak, aye, awọn ilana ti kii ṣe Gẹẹsi). Wọn ko to nikan fun ibamu. Tun awọn ipo pada ki o ṣe idanwo lodi si ijabọ rẹ ṣaaju gbigbekele wọn.

### Ibeere-ẹgbẹ: Ẹṣọ abẹrẹ kiakia

| Ayípadà                           | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                             | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                           |
| --------------------------------- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`         | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Jeki wíwo awọn ifiranṣẹ ti nwọle fun awọn ilana abẹrẹ kiakia.                                                                                                     |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`         | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Ilana abẹrẹ: `warn` = log nikan, `block` = kọ ibeere pẹlu 400. Legacy `redact` ṣe** kii ṣe** ọrọ abẹrẹ rinhoho; lo `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` fun ìbéèrè PII atunkọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Legacy inagijẹ fun `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` - iwa kanna.                                                                                                            |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`         | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Iyatọ ti o kere julọ ti `MODE=block` kọ: `high` (aiyipada), `medium`, tabi `low`. Awọn awoṣe alabọde jẹ akiyesi-nikan ayafi ti o ba lọ silẹ.                      |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Legacy inagijẹ fun `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` - iwa kanna.                                                                                                 |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`        | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Nigbati `true`, ṣe atunṣe PII ni awọn ibeere ti nwọle (laisi ipo abẹrẹ).                                                                                          |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`        | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Ṣe atunṣe awọn ilana API-bọtini / aṣiri ti a mọ daradara lati awọn ẹru isanwo request/response. Jade-ni; awọn digi `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                       |

### Idahun-Ẹgbẹ: PII Sanitizer

| Ayípadà                          | Aiyipada | Faili orisun              | Apejuwe                                                                 |
| -------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`  | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Ṣe ayẹwo awọn idahun LLM fun PII ti o jo ṣaaju ki o to pada si alabara. |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact` | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = mask PII, log `warn` = nikan, `block` = ju gbogbo esi silẹ.  |

### VS Code Tokenized-Route Context Sanitizer

| Ayípadà                             | Aiyipada | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ----------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`      | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Ipinnu ọrọ-ọrọ olootu ti nṣiṣe lọwọ (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) lati awọn ibeere `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ati ṣe atunṣe akoonu ti awọn faili ifarabalẹ ti o somọ ni gbangba. Ni aabo-nipasẹ-aiyipada; ṣeto si `0` lati mu. |

### Awọn oju iṣẹlẹ

| Oju iṣẹlẹ           | Iṣeto ni                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ibamu ile-iṣẹ**   | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (awọn bulọọki abẹrẹ + request/response PII atunṣe; awọn ipo jẹ ominira) |
| **Abojuto nikan**   | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — awọn akọọlẹ ṣugbọn kii ṣe awọn bulọọki                                                                                                 |
| **Lilo ti ara ẹni** | Fi gbogbo alaabo silẹ - odo lori oke                                                                                                                                                                 |

## 6. Irinṣẹ & Awọn ilana Ilana

| Ayípadà                                                     | Aiyipada                     | Faili orisun                                    | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Awọn iṣakoso LLM tool/function-calling wiwọle. `allowlist` = awọn irinṣẹ ti a ṣe akojọ nikan, `denylist` = gbogbo ayafi ti a ṣe akojọ, `disabled` = ko si awọn ihamọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Ona lati san awọn ofin ifọwọyi faili JSON (per-model/protocol upstream tweaks).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Atunkọ agbedemeji (ms) fun gbigba-gbigbona gbona faili awọn ofin isanwo. `1000` ti o kere ju.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Jade ni: ipa ọna igboro `claude-*` awoṣe IDs lati ọdọ awọn onibara koodu Claude nipasẹ akoto Claude Code OAuth dipo ti o nilo ìpele olupese. Awọn ìpele olupese olupese si tun bori. Paapaa atunto nipasẹ toggle dasibodu kan lori oju-iwe olupese Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Ṣeto `false` lati da OmniRoute kun fifi `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` Anthropic beta funrararẹ (awọn bulọọki ero ibuwọlu nikan). Awọn ibeere pẹlu `thinking.display: "summarized"` foo rẹ laibikita; beta-idunadura onibara-idunadura redact beta tabi CC-ibaramu `requestDefaults.redactThinking` ijade ntọju o. awọn ibeere Claude OAuth tọju beta (o jẹ apakan ti Claude Code OAuth itẹka) ayafi ti asia ẹya `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` aiyipada ba wa ni titan.                                                |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Legacy `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` ihuwasi (#4825): rọpo eyikeyi orukọ awoṣe igboro pẹlu `defaultModel` asopọ ti o yan. Paa nipasẹ aiyipada, `auto` nikan tabi awoṣe ti o ṣofo ti rọpo; ṣe igbasilẹ ikilọ kan fun bata nigbati o ba wa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Fun-awoṣe concurrency fila fun yika-robin combos (#9100). Semaphore-robin combo konbo jẹ lile-capped ni awọn ibeere igbakọọkan 3 fun awoṣe pẹlu ko si ifasẹyin, serializing ijabọ-concurrency giga lẹhin fila yẹn. Ti fọwọsi si `>= 1`, dimọ si `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Ijade-inu ti o lewu ti o fo OmniRoute's local context-window/max-input-token ayẹwo fun awọn ibeere awoṣe ẹyọkan taara. Awọn olupese ti o wa ni oke tun ṣe imudara awọn opin gangan wọn; funmorawon ni kiakia ati awoṣe ti ara ti o wu-àmi fila wa lọwọ. Iwaju imunadoko ni Awọn asia Ẹya DB danu > ayika oniyipada > aiyipada; ko si tun bere.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(a ko ṣeto) _               | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | YAML `providers:` inu ila (RIC-738, D4). Nigbati o ba ṣeto (pẹlu tabi laisi bulọọki `strategy:` ), `/v1/chat/completions` yipada si titẹ sii ibaramu OpenAI-ibaramu ti ararẹ dipo opo gigun ti awọsanma. Yọọ kuro (aiyipada): ipa ọna naa ṣubu taara si opo gigun ti awọsanma ti o wa. Wo `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(a ko ṣeto) _               | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Ọna si faili YAML kan ti o ni idaduro iwe `providers:` kanna bi `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, fun awọn imuṣiṣẹ ti o fẹran faili ju env var inline. Boya ọkan mu titẹ sii ti gbalejo ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(a ko ṣeto - ọna ṣiṣi)_     | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Bọtini API pínpín iyan fun titẹ sii ti a ti gbalejo ti iṣọkan (D5 scaffold, ti a fi pamọ fun eto ipin ipin-kọọkan). Nigbati o ba ṣeto, awọn ibeere gbọdọ ni `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Ti ko ṣeto: ipa-ọna naa wa ni sisi, ni ibamu pẹlu ilana olupese agbegbe ti ara ẹni ti o wa tẹlẹ ( imuṣiṣẹ loopback/trusted-network ).                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(a ko ṣeto) _               | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Inline YAML `strategy:` iwe fun awọn deterministic afisona engine (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, iye owo- ayo, lairi-mọ, fallback pq. Yipada idinamọ opopo `strategy:` ti o wa ni inu `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, fun-bọtini. Wo `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(a ko ṣeto) _               | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Ọna si faili YAML kan ti o ni idaduro iwe `strategy:` kanna bi `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, fun awọn imuṣiṣẹ ti o fẹran faili ju env var inline.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(a ko ṣeto) _               | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Ṣeto `1` lati da gbigba itan ibaraẹnisọrọ duro. `resolveConversationId()` da abajade ti ko tọpinpin pada ṣaaju ki o to ka SQLite tabi sọ itan-akọọlẹ ifiranṣẹ, ati igba ti a pese IDs ti alabara ti wa ni bo paapaa. Imudani ipa-ọna ko yipada ati pe awọn igbasilẹ ti o wa tẹlẹ ko jẹ paarẹ. Fun awọn imuṣiṣẹ ti ko lo wiwo ibaraẹnisọrọ dasibodu ati fẹ ki tabili titan duro lati da dagba.                                                                                                                                        |

Yiyan: awọn ipe ti ko ni bọtini ilẹ lori ipele ailorukọ Nadir, eyiti o jẹ opin-oṣuwọn fun IP ati nitorinaa ko ṣe nkan elo lẹhin ẹnu-ọna pinpin. Ni ifarabalẹ; fẹ aaye fun-konbo nigbati combos lo awọn akọọlẹ oriṣiriṣi.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Ipilẹ orisun ti Nadir ipinnu API, ti a lo nigbati konbo ti ara `config.nadir.baseUrl` jẹ unset. Nikan nilo fun Nadir ti o gbalejo ti ara ẹni, eyiti o tọju awọn olori kiakia inu nẹtiwọọki tirẹ. A fi aaye gba `/v1` itọpa ati yiyọ kuro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Fèrèsé ọ̀sẹ̀ fún àwọn ààlà ìṣàmúlò-bọtini-API-bọtini USD (ibere imuṣiṣẹ, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: ọsẹ naa pari ni ibẹrẹ ọsẹ akọkọ laarin awọn asopọ oke ti bọtini le de ọdọ, tabi jẹ awọn ọjọ 7 yiyi nigbati ko si ọkan ti a mọ. `calendar`: ọsẹ agbegbe lati Ọjọ Aarọ 00:00 si Ọjọ Aarọ ti nbọ 00:00, aami fun gbogbo bọtini. `calendar` ni a ṣe iṣeduro nigbati adagun kan ti awọn akọọlẹ oke ti pin nipasẹ ọpọlọpọ awọn bọtini isuna ti olukuluku. Awọn iye aiṣedeede ṣubu pada si `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(Aago ilana)_               | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | IANA timezone (fun apẹẹrẹ `Asia/Shanghai`) ti o ṣe asọye Ọjọ Aarọ 00:00 fun ferese ọsẹ `calendar`. Ai ṣeto tabi aiṣedeede: agbegbe aago ilana OmniRoute ( `TZ` ti agbalejo tabi eiyan, eyiti ko ṣeto nigbagbogbo, ie Aago Iṣọkan, ninu awọn aworan apoti). Aibikita ni ipo `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Nọmba ti o pọju aiyipada ti awọn irinṣẹ laaye ninu ibeere kan ( aiyipada OpenAI ). Awọn odidi rere nikan; aito tabi awọn iye aiṣedeede tọju `128`. Olupese ti o ni opin known/proactive (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) tun kan fila tirẹ - eyi nikan n gbe aja aiyipada lọ. Awọn ibi-afẹde ọna kika Claude ko ni fila aiyipada (Awọn ifiranṣẹ API ko ni nkankan); a kẹkọọ iye si tun kan.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(lori)_                     | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | SYNTX.ai ti n pe ohun elo ti o ni kiakia: unset tabi `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` nfi katalogi irinṣẹ agbegbe sinu itọsẹ; iye miiran (fun apẹẹrẹ `0`) fo abẹrẹ yẹn.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## 7. URLs & Awọsanma Sync

| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL -ẹgbẹ olupin fun awọn iṣẹ amuṣiṣẹpọ inu lati pe `/api/sync/cloud`. Jeki eyi jẹ loopback/container URL paapaa nigba ti ohun elo naa jẹ aṣoju ni gbangba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Kii ṣe eto akoko asiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Awọn liana ti wa ni parẹ ṣaaju ki o to Kọ. Kii ṣe eto akoko asiko.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Aiyipada jẹ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Ibuwọlu ti o wa ni nigbagbogbo jẹri - ati nigbagbogbo kọ nigbati `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` ko ṣeto - laibikita asia yii. Aiyipada yipada si imuse ni v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Ṣeto eyi nigbati OpenWebUI tabi isọdọtun miiran ba de OmniRoute nipasẹ URL inu ṣugbọn ẹrọ aṣawakiri olumulo gbọdọ mu media ti ipilẹṣẹ lati LAN, oju eefin, tabi orisun gbogbo eniyan. Ṣe** kii ṣe** pẹlu `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Ilana ti a lo nigbati o njade ohun itanna olupese ti n ṣafihan URL lati HOST/PORT laisi ipilẹṣẹ ibeere kan. Ṣeto si `https` lẹhin aṣoju-iduro TLS ti gbogbo eniyan nigbati ko si `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` ti o fojuhan ti ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ipo igbẹkẹle iyan fun awọn akọle ipilẹṣẹ ti gbogbo eniyan ti o dariji. Unset = ma ṣe gbẹkẹle `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` fun awọn ipinnu aabo. `true` / `loopback` gbẹkẹle host/proto ti a firanṣẹ siwaju nikan lati aami-ami-aṣoju loopback. `private` / `lan` tun gbẹkẹle awọn ẹlẹgbẹ aṣoju-LAN ikọkọ, ati pe o jẹ ki afara `API_PORT` tọju iru `X-Forwarded-For` ẹlẹgbẹ kan.
