Lori nipa aiyipada niwon # 10571; jade pẹlu `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Aṣoju Olumulo aiyipada ti a lo nigbati `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` ba wa ni titan ko si si olupilẹṣẹ `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` ti o ti ṣeto. Nikan lo si awọn olupilẹṣẹ ṣiṣi koodu. Lori ibeere ti ko ni bọtini kan awọn ẹnu-ọna oke, iye atunto ti ko gbe `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` ti rọpo nipasẹ aiyipada yii kuku ju kọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (lori)                                                | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Dari awọn akọle itọsi ohun elo alabara (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) si awọn olupese ti o ga, nitorinaa ibeere ti o bẹrẹ inu ijanu ti a ṣe akojọ jẹ ki idanimọ rẹ jẹ nipasẹ olulana (bọtini atẹjade OpenRouter lori iwọnyi). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` tọju idanimọ registry/operator ati ju awọn iye ti a pese fun alabara silẹ. Awọn akọle aṣa atunto onišẹ nigbagbogbo bori lori awọn iye alabara nigbagbogbo.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Iye fun akọsori `x-opencode-client` ti a ṣepọ nigbati `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` wa ni titan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Iye fun akọsori `x-opencode-project` ti a ṣepọ nigbati `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` wa ni titan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Eto ti o munadoko ti awọn olupese ti awọn ikuna oke rẹ jẹ bucketed nipasẹ egress IP (awọn papa itura 429 kan ni gbogbo pinpin asopọ arakunrin ti o IP). Unset nlo aiyipada ipele-ọfẹ `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` mu kasikedi naa kuro patapata; akojọ ti o ya sọtọ komama rọpo ṣeto. Awọn ero isanwo jẹ bucketed iroyin ni oke ati pe o le mu kuro lailewu (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Kòta-kiko egress iyipo dopin. Unset ṣeto ọmọ ẹgbẹ adagun kan si apakan lori 429 nikan fun idile IP-bucketed loke; `all` gbooro pe kikọ si gbogbo olupese, nitorinaa opin oṣuwọn ti o gba nipasẹ ọmọ ẹgbẹ kan ṣe yiyan yiyan atẹle ni ibomiiran. Eyikeyi iye miiran ju `all` ti wa ni bikita. Nilo `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (lori aiyipada). Ile itaja naa jẹ bọtini nipasẹ adirẹsi egress, kii ṣe (olupese, adirẹsi), nitorinaa awọn papa itura 429 ti o gbooro ti ọmọ ẹgbẹ fun gbogbo olupese titi akoko naa yoo fi kọja (iṣẹju 5, ilọpo meji si 15). Ka fun abajade esi, nitorinaa iyipada kan laisi atunbere. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Ṣeto si `off` lati da ṣiṣatunṣe ara awọn ibeere OpenCode ti ko ni bọtini (asia ṣiṣanwọle ati atokọ irinṣẹ). Awọn akọle tun wa ni lilo. Ka fun ibeere, nitorinaa iyipada kan yoo ni ipa lẹsẹkẹsẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Awọn orukọ irinṣẹ ti o ya sọtọ Comma lati kede lori ibeere OpenCode ti ko ni bọtini ti ko ni kankan, nigbati ko si ohun ti a ti ṣe akiyesi sibẹsibẹ fun awoṣe yẹn. Sofo ṣubu pada si ibi ipamọ kan ti a sọ fun awoṣe lati ma pe. Ni pupọ julọ awọn titẹ sii 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a ko bikita awọn titẹ sii ti ko tọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Iwe-ẹri ti a fi ranṣẹ si oju-ọna ipele ọfẹ ti o ni ibode (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Lati ọdun 2026-10-21 ọna yẹn ṣe idahun 403 `Model access is disabled` si bọtini API gidi kan ati pe o nṣe iranṣẹ gangan gangan dipo. `key` tọju bọtini API oniṣẹ, `none`/`off` ko firanṣẹ akọsori iwe-ẹri, eyikeyi ọrọ gangan ni a firanṣẹ bi ami ti o ru. Ka fun ìbéèrè.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ollama Awọn eto Awọsanma URL ti a lo fun fifin ipin. Daju fun relays / igbeyewo amuse.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                          | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Ollama Awọsanma ṣe akọsilẹ opin iwọntunwọnsi oke (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). Ka ni ẹẹkan ni fifuye module. Lo akọkọ fun Ollama Awọsanma ipin nigbati asopọ ba ni bọtini API ; scraper kuki oju-iwe awọn eto duro bi ipadabọ. Daju fun relays / igbeyewo amuse.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` kukisi ti a lo fun awọn eto-ipin ipin-iwe. Ni ifarabalẹ; fẹ aaye Dasibodu asopọ fun-asopọ nigbati ọpọlọpọ awọn akọọlẹ ti wa ni tunto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Alternate Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` kukisi env var. Ni ifarabalẹ; fẹ aaye Dasibodu asopọ fun-asopọ nigbati ọpọlọpọ awọn akọọlẹ ti wa ni tunto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                            | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Alternate Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` kukisi env var ti a lo ṣaaju awọn inagijẹ kukuru. Ni ifarabalẹ; fẹ aaye Dasibodu asopọ fun-asopọ nigbati ọpọlọpọ awọn akọọlẹ ti wa ni tunto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro) opin opin lilo lilo. Daju fun relays / igbeyewo amuse.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Nigbati o ba n ransẹ lẹhin aṣoju yiyipada (nginx, Caddy), ṣeto `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` si URL ti gbogbo eniyan iduroṣinṣin (fun apẹẹrẹ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) nigbati awọn ipe ipe OAuth tabi awọn ọna asopọ ti gbogbo eniyan ti ipilẹṣẹ gbọdọ lo orukọ olupin naa. Laisi eyi, awọn ipepada OAuth le kuna nitori redirect_uri ko ni baramu ati pe awọn ọna asopọ ti gbogbo eniyan ti ipilẹṣẹ le tọka si ipilẹṣẹ inu inu.
>
> Jeki `BASE_URL` bi loopback/container URL inu fun awọn iṣẹ olupin si olupin. Maṣe lo `Origin` aṣawakiri kan tabi orukọ agbalejo gbogbo eniyan fun awọn afọwọṣe ti ara ẹni ti o ni ẹri.
>
> Dasibodu ti a fọwọsi ko nilo ipilẹ ita gbangba URL: dasibodu naa nfiranṣẹ awọn ibeere ti ko ni aabo orisun-kanna pẹlu ami-ami CSRF ti igba kan. OmniRoute tun ṣe agbedemeji afọwọsi orisun ti gbogbo eniyan fun awọn iṣọpọ ẹrọ aṣawakiri ti kii-dasibodu: URL gbangba gbangba env vars ni igbẹkẹle akọkọ; raw `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` afori jẹ aibikita ayafi ti `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ti ṣiṣẹ ati pe ẹlẹgbẹ aṣoju lẹsẹkẹsẹ jẹ ami ami-ami gẹgẹbi igbẹkẹle. Maṣe lo awọn eto CORS lati ṣatunṣe awọn ibeere dasibodu orisun-kanna; CORS jẹ nikan fun awọn onibara aṣawakiri orisun-agbelebu.

---

## 8. Aṣoju ti njade

Awọn ipe olupese LLM ti o wa ni oke nipasẹ HTTP tabi aṣoju SOCKS5 fun iṣakoso egress, ipa-ọna-ilẹ, tabi iboju-boju IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` aiyipada si `true`. Ṣeto si `false`, `0`, `no`, tabi `off` lati fi agbara mu HTTP/1.1 lori Undici dispatchers ni `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` ati `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: taara egress, HTTP(S)/SOCKS proxies, relays, ati titun-socket retries. Eyi ṣe itọju ijẹrisi TLS, ijẹrisi aṣoju, awọn opin asopọ, ati awọn eto mimu-laaye. Tun OmniRoute bẹrẹ lẹhin iyipada nitori pe awọn olufiranṣẹ ti wa ni ipamọ ati pe awọn aṣoju isọdọtun ti ṣẹda ni ibẹrẹ. Yipada naa ko ṣakoso titẹ ika ọwọ wreq TLS tabi awọn gbigbe ti olupese kan pato.

| Ayípadà                                  | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                                   | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`         | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mu aṣoju SOCKS5 ṣiṣẹ fun awọn ipe oke. Jade kuro pẹlu `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`         | Onibara-ẹgbẹ                                   | Imọ-ẹgbẹ alabara ti wiwa SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`         | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Lori-nipasẹ-aiyipada ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; dasibodu DB bori bori). Awọn adagun-aṣoju ati yiyi-iroyin kan duro lati tun sin ọmọ ẹgbẹ kan ti o kan kuna (kiko ibere TCP, tabi 429 nipasẹ rẹ) fun akoko ti o ni ilọpo meji lori atunwi kọọkan, titi de fila kan. `false` ṣe atunṣe yiyan itele.                                                 |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Gbigba-akojọ fun awọn ibi-aṣayan iṣakoso agbegbe-mojuto ti o kọja loopback (comma/space awọn ogun ti o yapa tabi IPs, `*` ṣi LAN/private ṣugbọn ko ṣe metadata/link-local). Loopback lori http/https jẹ idasilẹ nipasẹ aiyipada. A dasibodu DB iye AamiEye lori yi ayípadà nigba ti ṣeto ati ti kii-sofo.                                                                    |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`       | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Akoko mimọ (ms) ti 429 ṣeto-ẹgbẹ, ilọpo meji lori kọọkan tun. Ka ni ẹẹkan ni ibẹrẹ. Ti a dè si `1000..3600000`; jade-ti-ibiti o tabi unreadable ṣubu pada si awọn aiyipada pẹlu kan ìkìlọ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`       | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Fila (ms) ti 429 ṣeto-ẹgbẹ. Ka ni ẹẹkan ni ibẹrẹ. Ti a dè si `1000..3600000`; jade-ti-ibiti o tabi unreadable ṣubu pada si awọn aiyipada pẹlu kan ìkìlọ. Nigbati fila ba wa ni isalẹ ipilẹ, o ṣubu pada si aiyipada. Sokale fila tun kuru igbagbe ṣiṣan: awọn titẹ sii wẹ `2 x max` lẹhin ipari.                                                                             |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`        | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Jade-ni ẹya asia (wo [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md) ; a dasibodu DB bori bori). Fun awọn olupese ti ipin wọn jẹ bucketed nipasẹ adiresi egress, ipo ọmọ ẹgbẹ adagun kan pinpin adirẹsi egress ọmọ ẹgbẹ laipẹ kọ ni isalẹ awọn ọmọ ẹgbẹ ilera. Paṣẹ nikan, ko yọkuro rara. Nilo `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, eyiti o ṣe agbejade ifihan agbara ti o ka.              |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`       | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Ferese rebound (ms) capping tun `proxy.set_aside` webhook titaniji fun ọmọ ẹgbẹ adagun-odo kan nitoribẹẹ ti awọn ijusile ti nfi iṣẹlẹ kan ranṣẹ. Ka lori kọọkan emit. Ti a dè si `1000..3600000`; jade-ti-ibiti o tabi unreadable ṣubu pada si awọn aiyipada ni ipalọlọ.                                                                                                     |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | Node.js bošewa                                 | HTTP aṣoju fun awọn ipe oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | Node.js bošewa                                 | HTTPS aṣoju fun awọn ipe oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | Node.js bošewa                                 | Aṣoju gbogbo agbaye (ṣe atilẹyin `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Pin ibi-afẹde iwoyi-IP ti a lo nipasẹ awọn iwadii egress aṣoju si URL kan. Ti ko ṣeto, iwadii naa gbiyanju `api64.ipify.org` lẹhinna `api4.ipify.org` nitorina awọn oju eefin IPv4-nikan ko royin pe o ti ku (#9694).                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | Node.js bošewa                                 | Koma-yaya hostnames/IPs lati fori awọn aṣoju.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`           | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | O pọju awọn sockets nigbakanna fun cache HTTP/SOCKS aṣoju dispatcher. Awọn ṣiṣan SSE ti o pẹ gẹgẹbi Codex `/v1/responses` nilo asopọ diẹ sii ju ọkan lọ nigbati ọpọlọpọ awọn ibeere pin aṣoju ipele-iroyin kan naa. Awọn iye loke `256` ti wa ni capped.                                                                                                                     |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`        | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | SOCKS5 ìfọwọ́sowọ́pọ̀ (asopọ̀) àkókó ní ms. Gbe soke nigbati agbalejo ẹnu-ọna ibugbe ẹyọkan ti kọlu nipasẹ ibaramu giga (fun apẹẹrẹ 100 awọn ibeere igbakana) - ifọwọwọ gidi le kọja 10s labẹ adagun ti o kun bi o tilẹ jẹ pe aṣoju jẹ eyiti o le de ọdọ, eyiti bibẹẹkọ da lori bi `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` eke. Ti gbe ni `120000`.                                |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`        | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Nigbati `false` (aiyipada), ibeere ti aṣoju ti a yàn ti kuna lati yanju ni** kọ (kuna-ni pipade)** kuku ju ja bo pada si kan taara asopọ - idilọwọ awọn gidi-IP jo. Ṣeto `true` lati mu pada sẹhin DIRECT ogún.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`        | `open-sse/executors`                           | Spoof TLS itẹka nipa lilo wreq-js (mimics Chrome 124). Counters JA3/JA4 ìdènà.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Akojọ iyọọda olupese ti o ya sọtọ komama fun itọsọna TLS proxied tuntun (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). Taara TLS ntọju ihuwasi rẹ julọ nigbati a ko ṣeto; Awọn olupese wọnyi nikan ni ipa ọna nipasẹ afara itẹka Chrome-124. Groq (`api.groq.com`) ko yọkuro paapaa nigba ti atokọ iyọọda ko ṣeto, nitori Chrome JA3 nfa Cloudflare 1010 lakoko ti undici abinibi de API. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`        | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Gba aaye aṣawakiri Claude Turnstile Playwright laaye lati foju foju kọ awọn aṣiṣe ijẹrisi HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Awọn oju iṣẹlẹ

| Oju iṣẹlẹ                               | Iṣeto ni                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 nipasẹ oju eefin SSH**         | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Aṣoju HTTP ile-iṣẹ**                  | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Anti-ika ika**                        | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` - nbeere `wreq-js` (pẹlu)                                                                   |
| **Egress-dari / ko si taara wiwọle**    | Fi `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` silẹ (aiyipada). Awọn ibeere kuna lile nigbati aṣoju ko si dipo jijo nipasẹ taara.             |
| **Legacy / dev - gba ipadasẹhin taara** | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Mu pada ihuwasi lile ṣaaju: asopọ taara ti a lo nigbati ipinnu aṣoju kuna.                        |

> **Akiyesi (NVIDIA afọwọsi fori - #3226):** NVIDIA's API-bọtini ìmúdájú ibùdó ibi ìgbẹ́kẹ̀gbẹ́ nígbà tí a bá mú wá lágbàáyé proxy/TLS-patched ( unici dispatcher → 504). `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` mọọmọ fori patch aṣoju fun ipe afọwọsi kan ni lilo `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`. Eyi jẹ akọsilẹ, iyasọtọ ti o ni iwọn - o ṣe** kii ṣe** ipa chat/usage egress. Ikọja naa jẹ alapin-pin nipasẹ `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts`.

---

## 9. CLI Ọpa Integration

Ṣakoso bi OmniRoute ṣe ṣe awari ati ṣe ifilọlẹ CLI sidecars (koodu Claude, Codex, ati bẹbẹ lọ).

| Ayípadà                           | Aiyipada                         | Faili orisun                                          | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = wiwa PATH; `manual` = lo awọn ọna ti o fojuhan nikan.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(a ko ṣeto) _                   | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Awọn titẹ sii PATH ni afikun fun iṣawari alakomeji CLI (ipinya-alakan).                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(a ko ṣeto) _                   | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Daju iwe ilana ile fun kika awọn atunto CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Gbọdọ jẹ pipe ati inu ilana ile -** tabi** , ninu apo eiyan, ọna ti a fi dipọ (iyẹn bawo ni `/host-home` ṣe n ṣiṣẹ). Ohunkohun miiran ṣubu pada si dir ile. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Gba OmniRoute laaye lati kọ awọn faili atunto CLI (itura ami, data igba). Ṣeto si `false` lati jẹ ki gbogbo atunto CLI kọ kuna pẹlu aṣiṣe “kọ alaabo” ti o fojuhan.                                                                |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si Droid CLI alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, lẹhinna `cursor`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si alakomeji oluranlowo Cursor. Laisi rẹ, iṣawari gbiyanju `agent` akọkọ ati ki o ṣubu pada si `cursor`.                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji 5dive CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | 5dive's system state dir (awọn profaili auth ini root); digi 5dive ti ara STATE_DIR aiyipada.                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa lati Tẹsiwaju CLI alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si alakomeji koodu Qwen koodu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLI_AIDER_BIN`                   | `aider`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si alakomeji Aider CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_GOOSE_BIN`                   | `goose`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji Goose CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_WHYCODES_BIN`                | `whycodes`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji WhyCodes CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLI_GEMINI_BIN`                  | `gemini`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si alakomeji Google Gemini CLI - awọn sọwedowo detection/health -ẹgbẹ olupin nikan; `omniroute run gemini` ṣe ipinnu alakomeji `gemini` lati eto PATH.                                                                     |
| `CLI_KILO_BIN`                    | `kilocode`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ọna aṣa si koodu Kilo CLI alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_OPENCODE_BIN`                | `opencode`                       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ona aṣa si alakomeji OpenCode CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### CLI Profaili Auto-Sync

Awọn asia ẹya wọnyi wa ni ijade ati aiyipada. Wọn tun le yipada lati dasibodu koodu CLI.

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada | Faili orisun                                     | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Lẹhin imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe olupese kan, ṣe atunko awọn faili profaili `~/.codex/*.config.toml` laifọwọyi lati inu katalogi laaye. Nbeere `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ko ṣe ayipada atunto active/default Codex, auth, awọn eto Codex-lb, tabi yiyan olupese.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false`  | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Lẹhin imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe olupese kan, ṣe atunko awọn faili profaili koodu `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` Claude laifọwọyi lati inu katalogi laaye. O nilo `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ko ṣe ayipada atunto active/default Claude, auth, tabi yiyan olupese. |

### Docker Apeere

```bash
# Mount host binaries into the container and tell OmniRoute where they are:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` nikan yoo ni ipa nigbati ọna naa ba jẹ asopọ gangan lati inu agbalejo - so pọ pẹlu awọn gbeko bii `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (wo profaili `host` ni `docker-compose.yml`). Ọ̀nà tí kò sí nínú ilé aṣàmúlò eiyan tàbí òkè ìdìpọ ni a kò kọbi ara sí, nítorí kíkọ̀ níbẹ̀ yóò dànù nígbà tí a bá tún eiwé náà ṣe.

Aworan naa nṣiṣẹ bi `USER node`, nitorinaa `/root` ti a ko gbe ni** kii ṣe** a wulo danu.

| Ayípadà                                  | Aiyipada      | Faili orisun                        | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------- | ------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(laifọwọyi)_ | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Fi agbara mu wiwa apoti (`1`/`true`) tabi pipa (`0`/`false`). Ti nilo nikan ni awọn akoko ṣiṣe ni wiwa aifọwọyi padanu.                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`       | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Gba CLI-tool konfigi kọ sinu ohun unmounted eiyan ona lonakona. CLI deede jẹ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                                          |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | unset         | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Docker aworan nikan: Dasibodu** Ṣe imudojuiwọn Bayi** kọ ibeere JSON kan si faili yii (gbọdọ wa ni inu `DATA_DIR`) fun oluṣọ ẹgbẹ-ogun lati fa ati tun ṣe apoti naa. Wo [itọsọna Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### CLI Alakomeji (`omniroute`) awọn oluranlọwọ

Awọn oniyipada wọnyi ṣe atunṣe ihuwasi alakomeji `omniroute` CLI (kii ṣe iwari ẹgbẹ ẹgbẹ loke).

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada                          | Faili orisun                            | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(eto)_                           | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Fi agbara mu ede jade CLI. BCP-47 agbegbe (fun apẹẹrẹ `en`, `pt-BR`). Paarẹ eto agbegbe env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Ṣeto si `1` lati dari stdout/stderr olupin si ebute ni ipo abojuto. Dogba si asia `--log` lori `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Aami-ifọwọsi ẹrọ itasi bi akọsori `x-omniroute-cli-token`. Ti ipilẹṣẹ aifọwọyi ni iṣẹ-ṣiṣe 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Gbongbo ile Gemini ti o duro; ọkan-shot run nlo kan ibùgbé sọtọ ile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Akoko oore-ọfẹ ni awọn iṣẹju-aaya ṣaaju ipa-ipa ọmọ CLI ti o ni ami.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Ṣeto si 1 pẹlu DATA_DIR ti o fojuhan lati fo faili ayika-itọsọna data olumulo aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Àmi ìsiṣẹpọ fún `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` gba àmi ìsiṣẹpọ kan, kii ṣe bọtini API kan). Ni deede si `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Igba akoko-akoko HTTP (ms) fun awọn ibeere olupin CLI →.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Akoko ti o pọju (ms) CLI n duro de aaye ipari ilera olupin ṣaaju titẹ ikilọ akoko ipari kan. Wulo fun awọn ibẹrẹ otutu ti o lọra (fun apẹẹrẹ Windows). Tun ṣeto nipasẹ `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Ṣeto si `1` lati tẹ awọn iwadii retry/backoff si stderr lakoko awọn pipaṣẹ CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Itọsọna aṣa fun iṣawari ohun itanna CLI (awọn idii `omniroute-cmd-*` ). Aiyipada si `~/.omniroute/plugins/` nigbati a ko ṣeto. CLI-nikan - ko de ẹrọ iwoye ohun itanna ẹgbẹ olupin rara, eyiti o tọka nipasẹ `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` (apakan 2).                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(a ko ṣeto) _                    | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Pa awọn sọwedowo imudojuiwọn nigbati o ba ṣeto si eyikeyi iye ti ko ṣofo: akiyesi CLI ni ijade ilana ati dasibodu “imudojuiwọn ti o wa” asia (`GET /api/system/version` lẹhinna fo npm, GitHub ati awọn wiwa iroyin). Akiyesi CLI naa tun wa ni pipa ni CI, ipo idakẹjẹ, ati abajade ti kii ṣe ọrọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(ti ari)_                        | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Liveness URL wadi nipa `omniroute doctor`. Aṣayan `livenessUrl` eto kan gba iṣaaju; bibẹkọ ti yi iye ti wa ni lo verbatim. Ti ko ṣeto, CLI n gba aaye ipari `/api/health/degradation` dasibodu agbegbe naa.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ona katalogi olupese ti a lo nipasẹ awọn aṣẹ olupese CLI. Aṣayan `catalogPath` eto kan gba iṣaaju. Awọn ọna pipe ni a lo bi-jẹ; ojulumo ona yanju lati root ise agbese. Ti a ko ṣeto, CLI naa nlo itọsọna awọn iduro nigbagbogbo ti olupese, pẹlu faili `providers.ts` julọ bi ipadasẹhin.          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(aiyipada olupese)_              | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Awoṣe isubu agbaye fun awọn idanwo asopọ olupese CLI. Iṣaju iṣaju jẹ atunto awoṣe asopọ, awoṣe olupese-pato ti dopin, iye yii, lẹhinna awoṣe idanwo ti a ṣe sinu olupese.                                                                                                                           |

## 10. Ti abẹnu Aṣoju & MCP Integration

| Ayípadà                                         | Aiyipada                                            | Faili orisun                                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | auto-ri                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL ti o fojuhan fun awọn irinṣẹ MCP/A2A lati de ọdọ OmniRoute. Nfori wiwa aifọwọyi localhost.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                      | MCP/A2A modulu                                              | bọtini API fun ohun elo MCP inu ati awọn ipe ogbon A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                      | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | Bọtini ID fun ikasi iwe iṣayẹwo MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                      | Legacy                                                      | Inagijẹ Legacy fun `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Awọn ọjọ ti itan-ṣiṣe iṣẹ-ṣiṣe A2A ti a fipamọ sinu ibi ipamọ data agbegbe ṣaaju ki o to sọ di mimọ lojoojumọ paarẹ ọna kan. Ti ko ṣeto, kii ṣe nomba, tabi `<= 0` ṣubu pada si `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Pa-yipada fun A2A iranti-deba observability ẹya-ara. Ṣeto si `0` lati foju wiwa iranti iranti fun iṣẹ kan patapata; eyikeyi miiran iye (pẹlu unset) ntọju o sise.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Nṣiṣẹ Aṣoju Issue offline/local ti o gbasilẹ-ipin aaye ipari. Fi silẹ ni alaabo ayafi ti o ba n ṣiṣẹ ni gbangba ti o gbasilẹ-iṣisẹ iṣẹ agbegbe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                      | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Aago akoko (ms) fun Aṣoju Ọrọ kan ti o gbasilẹ-iṣiro ṣiṣe. Clamped si ohun ti abẹnu o pọju; ṣubu pada si aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu rẹ nigbati a ko ṣeto tabi aiṣedeede.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(ọrọ ti nṣiṣẹ)_                                    | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | CLI ọna jijin-ọna context/profile fun awọn aṣẹ `omniroute` ; dojuiwọn ọgangan ti nṣiṣe lọwọ ni ile itaja awọn ọrọ agbegbe. Ni deede si `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Pa ẹyìn `keytar` OS-keychain iyan fun awọn ẹrí ọrọ ọrọ CLI. Nigbati o ba ṣiṣẹ, awọn iwe-ẹri wa ni ipo `config.json` `0600` ati pe CLI njade ikilọ ipadasẹhin akoko kan; ti a pinnu fun ṣiṣe headless/container mọọmọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Fi agbara mu iṣakoso iwọle ti o da lori iwọn lori awọn ipe irinṣẹ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(gbogbo)_                                          | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Awọn aaye ti o ya sọtọ komama: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Only `1` registers it; anything else leaves it off.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Mu awọn iye ṣiṣẹ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Isuna owo-ori (ms) fun MCP hops ti o duro de olupese (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Dimọ si o kere `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Awoṣe katalogi amuṣiṣẹpọ ni awọn wakati.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oṣuwọn olupese-ipin-ipin ati aarin idibo ipin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Aafo (ms) laarin itẹlera OAuth iye owo ni amuṣiṣẹpọ olopobobo; awọn asopọ OAuth ni a mu ọkan ni akoko kan lati yago fun ti nwaye ni oke. `0` yọ kuro (ni igbakanna).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Min aarin (ms) laarin itẹlera oke iye owo lori ọna preflight/monitor fun-ibere; awọn aaye awọn ipe nẹtiwọọki nigbakanna ọpọlọpọ awọn akọọlẹ lori IP kan ko ti nwaye ni oke. Ti firanṣẹ sinu Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (awọn aaye mejeeji bu), OpenCode, ati awọn olutọpa ipin Crof (#6009, #6911). Ọna fifiranṣẹ jeneriki `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) ko tii bo — tọpinpin lọtọ. Kaṣe deba ko fowo. `0` alaabo; dimole `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Afikun aaye laileto fun ibeere kọọkan (ms), ti a ṣe ayẹwo ni wiwa ipin oke ti o wa ni ipamọ kọọkan. Awọn aṣiṣe si 120; clamped `0..60000`; `0` yọ nikan jitter. Ṣeto `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` lati mu gbogbo finasi kuro. Jitter ko ṣe iṣeduro idena ti awọn idinamọ oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### OAuth CLI (Inu)

| Ayípadà             | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                    | Apejuwe                                  |
| ------------------- | -------------- | ------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | auto-ri        | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Server URL fun CLI↔OmniRoute auth afara. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Auth àmi fun CLI Afara.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`          | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Olumulo ID fun awọn akoko afara CLI.     |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Inagijẹ Legacy fun `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`.   |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Inagijẹ Legacy fun `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.    |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Inagijẹ Legacy fun `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.  |

## 11. OAuth Awọn iwe-ẹri Olupese

Awọn iwe-ẹri ti a ṣe sinu fun** localhost idagbasoke** . Fun awọn imuṣiṣẹ latọna jijin, forukọsilẹ tirẹ ni console olupilẹṣẹ olupese kọọkan.

| Ayípadà                                     | Olupese                  | Awọn akọsilẹ                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | koodu Claude (Anthropic) | Gbangba onibara - ko si ikoko ti nilo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude koodu             | Pa àtúnjúwe URI. Aiyipada: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI           | Gbangba onibara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)          | Nbeere ibaamu `_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

Ti yanju nipasẹ `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Forukọsilẹ app ni `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` pẹlu àtúnjúwe URI `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` ati scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ṣubu pada si `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

Iyan - sisan PKCE ko nilo aṣiri kan. ṣubu pada si `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

Ṣubu pada si `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Ti a lo nigbati iyatọ `_DUO_` ko ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Unset (aiyipada) = ko si fila.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                    | `print` (aiyipada) spawns `qodercli --print` fun ìbéèrè; `acp` tọju ilana `qodercli --acp` itẹramọṣẹ kan fun PAT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                    | ACP irinna: pa awọn judercli ilana lẹhin ti yi Elo laišišẹ akoko. `600000` aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                    | ACP irinna: akoko ipari fun qodercli lati dahun ACP `initialize`. aiyipada `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                    | gbigbe ACP : tunlo ilana qodercli ti o tẹpẹlẹ lẹhin ọpọlọpọ awọn akoko yii. `500` aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web             | Iwaju ami `tk` lati firanṣẹ bi `validated` lori `/api/chat`. Ti beere nigbati Blackbox fi agbara mu ibaramu tokini; bibẹẹkọ OmniRoute ṣubu pada si UUID laileto. Wo atejade #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Iran Bridge guardrail    | OpenAI-ibaramu URL mimọ fun ti kii-Anthropic iran-afara awọn ipe. Awọn aiyipada si julọ OpenAI URL env tabi api.openai.com. Tọkasi ni OmniRoute's `/v1` ara-loop tabi eyikeyi OpenAI-compat endpoint (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Oro #2232. Nigbati URL ba jẹ OmniRoute ti ara `/v1`, ibeere iha-apejuwe naa nfi `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ranṣẹ ati pe o jẹri pẹlu iwe-ẹri ara-lupu ti o yanju (`sk_omniroute` sentinel ni ipo agbegbe, tabi `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) nitorinaa awọn imuṣiṣẹ `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` ṣiṣẹ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Iran Bridge guardrail    | bọtini API fun URL loke. Yipadanu fun olupese OpenAI / Google env vars fun awọn ipe afara iran ti kii-Anthropic. awọn awoṣe Anthropic tọju ọna bọtini Anthropic iyasọtọ wọn. Oro #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Iran Bridge guardrail    | Igba melo ni abajade ipa ọna “ko si oludije lilo” jẹ cache, ni ms (aiyipada `30000`). Awọn iye ti ko tọ tabi odi ṣubu pada si aiyipada; `0` kaṣe odi kuro. Orisun: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Lọ si [Google Cloud Console → Awọn iwe-ẹri](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials) 2. Ṣẹda OAuth 2.0 Client ID (iru: "Ohun elo wẹẹbu") 3. Fi URL olupin rẹ kun bi Aṣẹ àtúnjúwe URI 4. Rọpo awọn iye ijẹrisi ni `.env`.

## 12. Olupese Olumulo-Aṣoju danu

Pa akọsori `User-Agent` ti a fi ranṣẹ si olupese iṣẹ oke kọọkan. Eyi ni ipinnu ni agbara ni akoko asiko ṣiṣe nipasẹ kilasi ipilẹ alaṣẹ:

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Orisun:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Ayípadà | Aiyipada Iye | Nigbati lati mu imudojuiwọn |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Nigbati Anthropic ṣe idasilẹ ẹya CLI tuntun kan |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Ṣeto si `1`/`true` lati dari awọn orukọ ohun elo ijanu ẹni-kẹta lọrọ ẹnu si Anthropic lori awọn ipa ọna asopọ Anthropic mejeeji ( OAuth abinibi ati CLIProxyAPI). Nipa aiyipada executor deterministically aliases ti kii-Claude-Code ọpa awọn orukọ (Claude Code canonical map ibi ti ọkan wa, bibẹkọ ti PascalCase) ati ki o reverses wọn lori esi nipasẹ `_toolNameMap`, ki harnesses pẹlu snake_case irinṣẹ ti wa ni ko kọ bi itẹka ẹni-kẹta ibara. N ṣatunṣe aṣiṣe nikan. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Nigbati OpenAI ṣe imudojuiwọn Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Pa ẹyà onibara Codex ni ominira ti okun UA ni kikun |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Daju `x-grok-client-version` fun itọkasi, imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe, ati koodu ẹrọ OAuth, pẹlu aiyipada Olumulo Grok CLI -Aṣoju fun itọkasi ati imuṣiṣẹpọ awoṣe. Gba SemVer pẹlu metadata prerelease/build iyan, to awọn ohun kikọ 128 lẹhin gige gige aaye funfun agbegbe. Ti ko ṣeto, ofo, aiṣedeede, tabi awọn iye ti o ni laini-pipin lo pin ti a firanṣẹ. Ifitonileti kikun ti o wa tẹlẹ Olumulo-Aṣoju danu duro ṣaaju. |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Pa ẹyà koodu Claude ti a kede ni ominira ti `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic ibode diẹ ninu awọn awoṣe lori iye yii (# 12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Daju suffix awọn ohun kikọ 3 OmniRoute ti o somọ `cc_version=` ninu idina ìdíyelé Claude. Ijalu lẹgbẹẹ `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` - pinni ẹya nikan ṣe ipolowo bata `version.revision` ko si awọn itujade alakomeji gidi. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Fojusi ẹya Copilot CLI ti a polowo ni ominira ti `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Nigbati GitHub Copilot Chat imudojuiwọn |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Nigbati Antigravity IDE awọn imudojuiwọn |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Nigbati Kiro IDE awọn imudojuiwọn |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Yiyọkuro Kiro ohun elo awujọ-koodu `clientId` (id ita gbangba) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Jade ni kikun: ifiranṣẹ CRC ni kikun fun-fireemu kan lori ṣiṣan iṣẹlẹ Kiro (ṣatunṣe awọn ṣiṣan ibaje) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD fun Kiro gbese; ṣe iyipada awọn kirẹditi `meteringEvent` sinu idiyele ibeere fun gangan |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Kuna idahun Kiro kan ti o ṣe ijabọ ko si iwọn-kirẹditi dipo gbigbasilẹ ti ko ni idiyele |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Nigbati Qoder CLI awọn imudojuiwọn |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Nigbati Cursor imudojuiwọn |

> [!TIP]
> O le ṣafikun awọn ifagile Olumulo-Aṣoju fun** eyikeyi** olupese lilo ilana `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Awọn executor ìmúdàgba kọ env var orukọ.

## 13. CLI Fingerprint Ibamu

Nigbati o ba ṣiṣẹ, OmniRoute tun paṣẹ awọn akọsori HTTP ati awọn aaye ara JSON lati ba ibuwọlu gangan ti awọn irinṣẹ CLI osise. Eyi dinku eewu ti ṣiṣafihan akọọlẹ lakoko ti o tọju IP aṣoju rẹ.

**Orisun:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Fun Olupese

| Ayípadà                  | Muu ṣiṣẹ | Ipa                                  |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Mimics Codex CLI ìbéèrè Ibuwọlu      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Mimics Claude Code Ibuwọlu ibeere    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Mimics GitHub Copilot ìbéèrè Ibuwọlu |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Mimics Antigravity Ibuwọlu ibeere    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Mimics Cursor Ibuwọlu ibeere         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Mimics Kimi Ifaminsi ibeere Ibuwọlu  |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Mimics Kilo Code Ibuwọlu             |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Mimics Cline Ibuwọlu ibeere          |

### Agbaye

| Ayípadà          | Muu ṣiṣẹ | Ipa                                                             |
| ---------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Mu ibamu itẹka itẹka ṣiṣẹ fun** gbogbo** awọn olupese ni ẹẹkan. |

### Kimi Ifaminsi CLI idanimo idojuk

| Ayípadà                 | Aiyipada             | Faili orisun                             | Apejuwe                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Fojusi ẹya Kimi CLI ti a firanṣẹ lakoko awọn ipe OAuth/API.        |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(aiyipada ti a mu)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Pa ohun-elo Kimi ti o gba silẹ ID ti a lo ninu awọn akọle alabara. |

> [!NOTE]
> Ẹya yii n ṣiṣẹ lẹgbẹẹ Aṣoju Olumulo (§12). Eto ika ika ọwọ n ṣakoso pipaṣẹ akọsori ati pipaṣẹ aaye ara, lakoko ti Olumulo-Aṣoju danu mu okun UA kan pato. Mejeeji le ṣee mu ṣiṣẹ ni ominira.
