## 14. API Key Olupese

awọn bọtini API fun awọn olupese ti o lo ijẹrisi taara.** Eto ti o fẹ:** Dasibodu → Awọn olupese → Fi bọtini API kun.

Ṣiṣeto nipasẹ awọn oniyipada ayika jẹ yiyan fun Docker tabi awọn ifilọlẹ ori.

Àpẹẹrẹ ti a mọ: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Ayípadà            | Olupese                                                  |
| ------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                                 |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                               |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Ipilẹṣẹ API + Reader fallback)                  |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (inagijẹ fun `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)                  |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) awọn ifibọ + iwiregbe fallback |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (inagijẹ fun `GEMINI_API_KEY`)                    |

> [!NOTE]
> Awọn titẹ sii `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` static fun Groq, xAI, Mistral, Idamu, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, ati Qianfan ni a yọkuro ni v3.8.0 nitori akoko asiko ko ka wọn mọ - awọn olupese wọnyẹn gbarale Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / ti paroko DB. Wo apakan _Audit: Yọ / Dead Variables_ apakan ni isale iwe yii fun ọna ijira.

> [!TIP]
> Awọn bọtini ti a ṣeto nipasẹ Dasibodu ti wa ni ipamọ ti paroko ni SQLite ati ki o gba iṣaaju lori awọn oniyipada ayika.
>
> **Jina:** `jina-ai/…` ifibọ, rerank, ṣe lẹtọ, apa, ati `jina-search` ṣe** kii ṣe** ṣowo iṣupọ env bọtini nigbati dashboard `jina-ai` (tabi pín `jina-reader`) asopọ wa — `getProviderCredentials` jẹ kikun-akọkọ. `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` ni a lo nikan nigbati ko si bọtini dasibodu ohun elo ti o wa. Awọn ipe ipe ṣe ifẹhinti env bi `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadi Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) ko sin `/v1/embeddings` tabi `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (inagijẹ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) nlo asopọ `gemini` dashboard akọkọ. `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` ni a lo nigbati ko si bọtini dasibodu ti o ṣee lo wa. Awọn ipe ipe ṣe ifasilẹ env bi `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Ijabọ multimodal abinibi nlo `x-goog-api-key` lodi si `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — N OpenAI `input` awọn ohun di N fekito.

---

## 15. Aago Eto

Gbogbo awọn iye wa ninu** millise seconds** . Ipinnu aarin ni `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Logalomomoise Timeout

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (global override)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (upstream provider calls, default: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (independent, default: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (independent, default: 0 = off, suggested: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (independent, default: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent, default: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (independent; unset: 1260000, raised by declared budgets; set: hard cap; 0 disables)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (caps adaptive readiness extensions, default: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (inherits from REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, default: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (derived, default: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (default: 0 = disabled)
```

| Ayípadà                                         | Aiyipada                                                          | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                    | Ọna abuja agbaye - dojukọ mejeeji `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` ati awọn aiyipada `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Lapapọ akoko akoko ibeere HTTP fun awọn ipe olupese ti oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Idakẹjẹ ti o pọju laarin awọn baiti oke aise ṣaaju iṣẹyun. Awọn awoṣe ironu gbooro ṣọwọn da duro > 90s.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | O pọju lapapọ lọwọ ṣiṣan SSE ṣiṣan igbesi aye; ko tunto lori awọn baiti oke ati pe o jẹ ominira ti `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Nigbati a ko ba ṣeto, aiyipada jẹ eyiti o tobi julọ fun apẹẹrẹ `timeoutMs` ninu iforukọsilẹ (1200000, Codex) pẹlu ala 60000 kan, ati ibi-afẹde kan ti o kede isuna to gun (asopọ `timeoutMs`, lẹhinna fun-apẹẹrẹ, lẹhinna ipele olupese, fun apẹẹrẹ GLM's 3000000) gba isuna yẹn dipo 60000. Iye rere jẹ fila lile fun gbogbo ibi-afẹde ti awọn eto isuna ti a kede ko le gbega. Ṣeto si `0` lati mu ṣiṣẹ fun gbogbo ibi-afẹde.                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(alaabo)_                                                        | Boya OmniRoute le gbejade awọn laini asọye SSE `:` (fun apẹẹrẹ `: keepalive` heartbeat ati awọn tirela metadata `x-omniroute-*` ). Alaabo nipasẹ aiyipada (# 10524) nitori awọn alabara ibaramu OpenAI ti o muna JSON.parse gbogbo laini SSE ati jamba lori awọn asọye `:` ; `data:` heartbeats ko ni ipa. Ṣeto `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` lati jade pada si. Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (pa)                                                          | Jade wọle: dapọ awọn iṣẹlẹ Claude `content_block_delta` itẹlera ( atọka bulọọki kanna ati iru delta) ti a firanṣẹ si awọn alabara Claude, ṣiṣan ni o kere ju gbogbo N ms. Ainipadanu; `signature_delta` ati awọn iṣẹlẹ aala ko dapọ rara. Kan si gbogbo iru delta ayafi ti a ti ṣeto oniyipada iru-iru kan ni isalẹ. Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (pa)                                                          | Ni iru-aparun `SSE_COALESCE_MS` fun `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (pa)                                                          | Ni iru-aparun `SSE_COALESCE_MS` fun `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (pa)                                                          | Ni iru-aparun `SSE_COALESCE_MS` fun `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (pa)                                                          | Jade wọle: awọn fireemu SSE ipele ti a firanṣẹ laarin N ms sinu kikọ kan si alabara. Awọn fireemu akọkọ ati awọn fireemu ebute (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) ko ni idaduro rara; pẹlu batching lori, ITL ṣe iwọn awọn ela ipele. Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | imuse oluṣọ Byte-stall fun awọn idahun ṣiṣanwọle. `timer` tun-apa kan aago kan fun ṣoki ti oke (ina ni pato ni isuna da duro); `interval` tọju ayẹwo kan fun ṣiṣan pẹlu akoko `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, nitorinaa da duro duro laarin akoko + isuna. Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Akoko lati gba iṣẹlẹ akọkọ ti kii ṣe ping SSE. Jogun `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` nigba ti ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Ṣeto `false`/`0`/`off` lati mu wiwa ni kikun kuro - awọn akoko imurasilẹ ati imularada ṣiṣan ko ni ga soke nipasẹ ibeere body/headers, idinku imudara akoko iṣakoso alabara-dari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Ferese imurasilẹ iṣẹlẹ akọkọ ti o pọju fun awọn ṣiṣiṣẹ aṣoju `/goal` ti a rii tabi awọn ibeere fi agbara mu pẹlu `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Mu imularada ṣiṣan ni kutukutu ṣiṣẹ laifọwọyi fun ṣiṣe aṣoju `/goal` ti a rii. Ṣeto `false`/`0`/`off` lati mu ijade-ibi-afẹde kan kuro. Eyi le gba imularada ADD nikan ni oke ti aiyipada oniṣẹ - ko daaju ijade kuro ni awọn eto `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB ti o fojuhan.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Yọ awọn iṣẹlẹ `codex.*` SSE ti kii ṣe boṣewa (fun apẹẹrẹ `codex.rate_limits`) ti o fọ OpenAI SDK's `responses.stream()` pẹlu 502. ON aiyipada (#11014). Ṣeto `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` lati dari wọn.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                    | Jáde-ni Codex app-server irinna. aaye ipari WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) ti ẹgbẹ ẹgbe `codex app-server` agbegbe kan. Fun-asopọ yi danu: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Awọn iṣe bi ilẹ-ilẹ: idaduro ti o munadoko jẹ tobi ti iye yii ati `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` tabi iye aiṣedeede yọ ilẹ-ilẹ kuro. Awọn agbalejo latọna jijin ko ni ipa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

Ko gba laaye ni isalẹ alapin pakà loke; nigbati olupe ko pese ifihan akoko ipari rara, atungbiyanju yoo jẹ ki ilẹ alapin ko yipada. |

Ṣiṣan Awọn idahun ṣi pẹlu `response.created`, nitorina ipalọlọ ti o kọja window yii jẹ iduro: akọọlẹ naa ti tutu ati pe ibeere naa yi pada si akọọlẹ atẹle ni ẹẹkan; a keji da duro kuna sare. `0` mu oluso kuro paapaa pẹlu asia lori.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Relay ti a fikọ gbọdọ kuna ṣaaju akoko asiko client/agent (~ 30s) nitorinaa awọn olupe rii ikuna kan pato-pada dipo akoko isunmọ oke jeneriki. Capped ni `29000` ki o nigbagbogbo ina akọkọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Pipin tun gbiyanju ifẹhinti fun direct/relay/proxy tun gbiyanju-ẹẹkan ni `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = tun gbiyanju lẹsẹkẹsẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ore-ọfẹ akoko ipari-lile JS ti a ṣafikun lori oke akoko asiko abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Ibere akoko asiko wreq-js abinibi (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ore-ọfẹ akoko ipari-lile JS ti a ṣafikun lori oke akoko asiko abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (pa)                                                          | Appends "O ti ṣe sinu ayelujara search. Dahun ibeere taara lilo awọn èsì àwárí." si ifiranṣẹ eto olupe (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). Paa nipasẹ aiyipada - Awọn wiwa idamu lọnakọna, ati pe gbolohun naa n jo sinu awọn idahun bi asọye-meta fun awọn alabara ifaminsi. Ṣeto `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` lati mu pada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ore-ọfẹ akoko ipari-lile JS ti a ṣafikun lori oke akoko asiko abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Ibere wreq-js abinibi akoko ipari (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` gbe soke fun ibeere lati `180000` fun awọn iran ti o gun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ore-ọfẹ akoko ipari-lile JS ti a ṣafikun lori oke akoko asiko abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Adagun aṣawakiri Playwright ti o pin fun iwiregbe kuki wẹẹbu ti o ṣe atilẹyin aṣawakiri (`browserPool.ts`); ṣeto `off` lati mu ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                    | Tọkasi ni Obscura (`http://host:port`) ti nṣiṣẹ tẹlẹ dipo sisọ ọkan; module ko ni ara ti ilana (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Ibudo ti o han gbangba fun `obscura serve` spawn; a yan ibudo ọfẹ laifọwọyi nigbati a ko ṣeto (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Jade ibeere iwiregbe kuki wẹẹbu kan si ọna ti a ṣe atilẹyin ẹrọ aṣawakiri (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` lati mu ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | Ipilẹ URL fun Kimi Wẹẹbu (kimi.ai Connect-RPC ti kariaye) alaṣẹ (`kimi-web.ts`); idojuk nikan fun mirror/proxy endpoints.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Aaye ipari iwiregbe ni kikun fun oluṣakoso oju opo wẹẹbu Kimi (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(awari-laifọwọyi)_                                               | Ona si eto Chrome/Edge ṣiṣe fun Adobe Firefly ibanisọrọ aṣawakiri iwọle (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); idojuk fun-OS auto-iwari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ iṣẹjade adaduro_                                         | Kọ-akoko idojuk fun awọn standalone o wu liana je nipasẹ awọn ranse si-Kọ colocation igbese (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); kọ tooling, ko asiko isise.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

Awọn igbiyanju ibi-afẹde Konbo jogun akoko akoko ibeere oke ti o yanju (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, tabi `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` nigba ti o pese aifọwọyi bu). Ṣeto `targetTimeoutMs` sinu konbo, konbo aseku, tabi olupese idojuk nikan lati ṣe konbo fallback yiyara; awọn iye ti o wa loke akoko isunmọ oke lọwọlọwọ ni a fi kun si akoko isunmọ oke.

`comboTimeoutMs` jẹ isuna iṣọṣọ ogiri gbogbo-konbo lọtọ kọja awọn ibi-afẹde ikuna. Fi silẹ ni aiṣeto tabi `0` lati tọju aṣetunṣe ailopin (iduro-iduro hardcoded iṣẹju mẹwa 10 ni `comboPredicates.ts` ṣi wulo). Iye rere kan rọpo netiwọki aabo yẹn fun konbo naa. Jeki `comboTimeoutMs` gun ju `targetTimeoutMs` ki failover tun ni akoko lẹhin ibi-afẹde akọkọ ti o lọra.

## 16. Wọle

Eto gedu naa kọwe si mejeeji stdout ati awọn faili log ti yiyi. Gbogbo iṣeto ni a ka nipasẹ `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Ayípadà                                   | Aiyipada                   | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ipele akọọlẹ ti o kere julọ: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọna kika: `text` (eniyan-ṣeékà) tabi `json` (ti a ṣeto).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Kọ awọn akọọlẹ lati faili lẹgbẹẹ stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ọna faili Wọle (i ibatan si root akanṣe tabi `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Iwọn faili ti o pọju ṣaaju iyipo. Gba: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, tabi awọn baiti pẹtẹlẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Awọn ọjọ lati tọju awọn faili log log ti yiyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Awọn afẹyinti faili log yiyipo ti o pọju.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Awọn ọjọ lati tọju awọn titẹ sii request/call sinu ibi ipamọ data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Awọn titẹ sii ipe wọle ti o pọju ninu ifipamọ inu-iranti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Awọn ori ila ti o pọju ninu tabili `call_logs` SQLite ṣaaju ki o to pruning.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(a ko ṣeto) _             | Fi ipa mu ibeere alaye wọle si tan tabi pa, ti o bori eto dasibodu naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (wakati 1)       | Ọjọ ori ti o pọju fun awọn titẹ sii iforukọsilẹ ibeere alainibaba ti nṣiṣe lọwọ ṣaaju ṣiṣe mimọ inu-iranti.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Awọn alaye isunmọtosi ti o pọju (odidi to dara); fila kika: gba; awọn fila byte/map : fi sii.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Tọju awọn ege ṣiṣan ni awọn ohun-ọṣọ opo gigun ti epo nigbati `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Jade-inu (`true`) — pipa nipasẹ aiyipada lati fi disk pamọ.                                                                                                                                  |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Iwọn pipe pipe pipe log artifact iwọn ni KB nigbati `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Awọn ori ila ti o pọju ninu tabili `proxy_logs` SQLite ṣaaju ki o to pruning.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Fi client/egress IPs ati awọn ami-iṣaaju akọọlẹ sinu awọn akọọlẹ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Awọn igbasilẹ dashboard/database aṣoju-log n ṣe idaduro awọn alaye ni kikun.                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Ṣeto si `"true"` tabi `"1"` lati ṣe igbasilẹ `first_chunk_ms` (firanṣẹ ibere si baiti ara iwulo akọkọ) lori awọn ori ila aṣoju-log. Murasilẹ kọọkan soke ara ni a ọlẹ passthrough san ati abulẹ awọn kana ni kete ti awọn baiti de; pipa nipa aiyipada ki awọn idahun kọja nipasẹ untouched. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 min)            | Igba melo ni `src/lib/logRotation.ts` tun ṣayẹwo iwọn faili log ti nṣiṣe lọwọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ipari okun ti o pọju ni idaduro ni awọn ohun elo akọọlẹ iwiregbe (aiyipada 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Nọmba awọn ohun orun ti o wa ni idaduro lati iru nigbati o ba npa awọn fifuye akọọlẹ iwiregbe ge.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Ijinle itẹ-ẹiyẹ ti o pọ julọ ṣaaju ki awọn ẹru isanwo log iwiregbe ti ge ge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Awọn bọtini ohun ti o pọju ti o wa ni idaduro ni awọn fifuye akọọlẹ iwiregbe (0 = ailopin).                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Gbogbo iwọn ara request/response (KB) ṣaaju ki o to rọpo rẹ nipasẹ akopọ igboro dipo ẹda oniye kikun. Gbe eyi soke ti awọn ibaraẹnisọrọ aṣoju gigun ba fihan ibi ipamọ dipo awọn ifiranṣẹ gidi ninu dasibodu naa.                                                                            |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Nigbati o ba jẹ otitọ, `serializeArtifactForStorage` foju iwọn-ti o da lori iwọn. Ṣatunkọ nikan.                                                                                                                                                                                             |

## 17. Memory o dara ju

| Ayípadà                       | Aiyipada                                                                                  | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _auto_ (irin igboro);**`1024` ninu aworan Docker**                                        | **Ti ṣe iṣeduro** Docker/standalone V8 òkiti ààlà (MB). Nigbati a ko ṣeto, ti ṣe iwọn ni agbara (~ 35% ti eto RAM, dimọ si `[512, 4096]`); `512` jẹ ilẹ nikan nigbati iranti lapapọ ko le ka. Lori `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), an** fojuhan** iye ti wa ni appended bi `--max-old-space-size` ati** bori** lori asia òkiti NODE_OPTIONS rogbodiyan (V8 kẹhin-flag). `omniroute serve` tun fẹran òkiti NODE_OPTIONS ti o wa tẹlẹ (#5238). Maṣe ṣeto awọn mejeeji si awọn nọmba oriṣiriṣi - ilana naa ṣe igbasilẹ ikilọ kan lorukọ awọn iye mejeeji ati olubori.** Aworan Docker osise nigbagbogbo n ṣeto `1024`, nitorinaa isọdiwọn ko ṣiṣẹ nibẹ.** Ifaminsi-oluranlowo `/v1/responses` nilo `8192`–`12288` plus cgroup headroom — wo [Docker Itọsọna — asiko isise RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; pipa nigbati `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB` ko ṣeto) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Ilana pipe-fiusi RSS (MB) fun iranti native/external ti V8 `heapUsed` ko le ri; loke rẹ awọn ibeere titun gba 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS ṣọwọn silẹ lẹhin iwasoke, nitorinaa so pọ pẹlu `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                      | Ti o pọju awọn titẹ sii tọ eto cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                          | Iwọn kaṣe kiakia lapapọ lapapọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (5 min)                                                                          | Titẹ sii kaṣe kiakia TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                     | Iwọn otutu ti o pọju = 0 awọn idahun.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                          | Iwọn kaṣe atunmọ lapapọ lapapọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                        | Titẹ sii kaṣe atunmọ TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                       | Awọn gbongbo agbegbe-corpus ti o tọju itọka iranti laaye ni ẹẹkan (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: ni opin iwọn atọka root ti o kere julọ-laipe-lo ti yọ kuro ati tun ṣe lori ibeere atẹle rẹ. Dimọ si o kere ju `1`; iye ti kii ṣe nomba ṣubu pada si aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                      | Awọn iṣẹlẹ ṣiṣan aipẹ ti o pọju ni ifipamọ wiwo ifiwe Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                                            | Ipari ọrọ-ọrọ agbaye-ipari (awọn ami ami) fun gbogbo olupese ti ko ni oniyipada `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Nigbati o ba ṣeto, o** rọpo** katalogi, dasibodu dasibodu ati awọn iye iforukọsilẹ fun awọn olupese wọnyẹn; nigbati a ko ṣeto, OmniRoute nlo pq ti a ṣe sinu rẹ, eyiti ibi-afẹde ikẹhin rẹ jẹ `128000`. Wo [Gigun ọrọ-ọrọ fun Olupese](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                     | Yara ori ami afikun ti o wa ni ipamọ nigba titọpa awọn ipin lilo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Gigun ọrọ-ọrọ fun Olupese (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) tun ka a** fun-olupese** fọọmu ti a kọ orukọ rẹ lati ọdọ olupese ID ni akoko asiko, nitorinaa ko le ṣe atokọ ni ila ni ila ninu tabili loke tabi ni `.env.example`.

**Oruko.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, nibiti `<PROVIDER>` ti jẹ olupese ID oke-cased, pẹlu gbogbo ohun kikọ ita `A-Z` / `0-9` rọpo nipasẹ `_`. Olupese ID jẹ ọkan ti o jẹ ilana lẹhin ipinnu inagijẹ (apakan `provider` ti okun `provider/model` ni kete ti inagijẹ kukuru ti ni ipinnu). Aṣa OpenAI- tabi awọn apa ibaramu Anthropic lo node ti ipilẹṣẹ ID wọn, nitorinaa UUID jẹ apakan ti orukọ oniyipada. Awọn iye ti wa ni a rere odidi nọmba ti àmi; ohunkohun miiran (ofo, `0`, odi, ti kii-nọmba) jẹ aibikita ati ipinnu gbe lọ si igbesẹ ti nbọ.

| Olupese ID                                                   | Orukọ oniyipada                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| oluranlowo olulana                                           | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                   | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-ibaramu-iwiregbe-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Iwaju.** Fun bata `provider/model`, igbesẹ akọkọ ti o mu iye kan yọrisi:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` fun olupese naa.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (agbegbe ayika agbaye).
3. Ferese fun-awoṣe: ipo-ọrọ ti o tẹsiwaju (ṣeto sinu dasibodu, tabi afọwọṣe-pin nipasẹ olulaja wiwa, wo `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), bibẹẹkọ katalogi amuṣiṣẹpọ / awọn awoṣe.dev iye.
4. Iforukọsilẹ olupese `defaultContextLength`.
5. Awoṣe-orukọ heuristics (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Awọn aṣiṣe ti a ṣe sinu fun olupese, lẹhinna `128000`.

Mejeeji ayika awọn igbesẹ ti waye si awọn** gbogbo olupese** : Gbogbo awoṣe ti olupese naa gba opin kanna, ati pe wọn bori lori awọn dasibodu awoṣe-apẹẹrẹ. Nigbati awoṣe kan ti olupese kan nilo window ti o yatọ, lo ifasilẹ fun-apẹẹrẹ ni dasibodu dipo.

**Ààlà.** Ẹwọn yii n ṣakiyesi akoko ibeere-akoko-window ( `context_length_exceeded` / “Input over context window” ijusile ni `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), awọn isuna funmorawon kiakia ati awọn opin ọrọ-ọrọ konbo. `context_length` ti a polowo nipasẹ `GET /v1/models` tun le wa lati inu iwe akọọlẹ tabi metadata iforukọsilẹ ati pe ko ṣe iṣeduro lati ṣe afihan ipadasẹhin ayika.

**Apeere.** Olupese ti awoṣe rẹ gba ferese ami-ami 1,050,000 nigba ti katalogi ṣe ijabọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Tun olupin bẹrẹ lẹhin iyipada rẹ: iye ti ka lati agbegbe ilana olupin. Lori awọn** tabili app** olupin naa nṣiṣẹ bi ilana ọmọde ati pe ko ri iyipada eto ti a ṣeto lẹhin ti a ti ṣe ifilọlẹ app; fi laini sinu faili `.env` awọn ẹru app (aṣẹ wiwa ni [itọsọna Electron : Ṣiṣayẹwo faili ayika](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup) ), lẹhinna dawọ kuro ni kikun ki o tun bẹrẹ app naa.

### Funmorawon

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada | Apejuwe                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | unset    | Gbẹkẹle ise agbese `.rtk/filters.json` laisi hash `.rtk/trust.json` kan. Lo nikan ni idagbasoke agbegbe ti iṣakoso. |

### Ẹrọ iranti (ètò 21)

### Iye owo lupu iṣẹlẹ ti iranti, awọn ọgbọn, ati isọdọtun ami (#10349)

OmniRoute ni a** nikan Node ilana** . extraction/retrieval iranti, abẹrẹ awọn ọgbọn, ati isọdọtun ami olupese ṣiṣe lori iyẹn** kanna iṣẹlẹ lupu** bi `GET /healthz` ati dasibodu. Wọn kii ṣe okun oṣiṣẹ.

| Ṣiṣẹ                       | Koodu                              | Aiyipada                                    | Iṣakoso onišẹ                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Iranti isediwon / igbapada | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dasibodu** Ti ṣiṣẹ iranti** (aiyipada lori) | Paa** Eto → Memory** . Nibẹ ni ko si lọtọ env pa yipada kọja disabling ẹya-ara ni eto.                                           |
| Ogbon abẹrẹ                | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dasibodu** Ti ṣiṣẹ ogbon** (aiyipada lori)  | Paa** Eto → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Awọn bọtini apoti iyanrin ni isalẹ ipaniyan ti a dè nikan lẹhin abẹrẹ ti wa tẹlẹ. |
| Isọdọtun ami               | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | Tan fun awọn olupese OAuth/web ti a ti sopọ | Ge asopọ olupese tabi jẹ ki awọn ami duro wulo; ko si `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` env loni.                                                |

Ti `/healthz` ba lọra lori apoti idakẹjẹ, mu awọn ọgbọn + iranti kuro ni akọkọ, lẹhinna ṣayẹwo fifuye catalog/compression (#10303, #9685). Awọn ẹya wọnyi mu jade ni awọn aaye `await` ṣugbọn ṣi dije fun okun kan.

Ifibọlẹ Layer, ibi-itaja fekito ati awọn bọtini atunto fun eto-iṣẹ iranti itẹramọṣẹ (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Ayípadà                              | Aiyipada                    | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (5 min)            | TTL fun kaṣe ifibọ inu-iranti (fun ibuwọlu source/model/dim ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Awọn titẹ sii LRU ti o pọju ti a fipamọ sinu kaṣe ifibọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id fun ijade-inu `@huggingface/transformers` agbegbe opo gigun ti MiniLM (~ 23 MB int8, ~ 400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id fun iṣipopada tabili wiwa potion/Model2Vec aimi. Ti ṣe igbasilẹ ni ọlẹ sinu kaṣe dir.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Liana ti a lo lati kaṣe awọn faili awoṣe potion aimi. Awọn aiyipada labẹ `DATA_DIR` nigbati a ko ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Paarẹ ipilẹ Ipele Oju-ọna Face Face URL ti a lo nipasẹ `staticPotion.ts` (fun apẹẹrẹ aaye ipari digi fun awọn iṣeto-afẹfẹ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Oke-K aiyipada ti a lo nipasẹ wiwa `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector inu `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Atunse ipo Fusion ibakan `k` fun arabara FTS5 + fekito igbapada (sqlite-vec ohunelo).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Test/diagnostic seaam in `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): nigbati `true`, fi agbara mu ibi-itaja vector lati `null` (ṣe afiṣe agbegbe cloud/WASM laisi `sqlite-vec`), imupadabọ iranti abuku si wiwa ọrọ-ọrọ FTS5. Fi aifọkanbalẹ silẹ ni iṣelọpọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(a ko ṣeto) _              | bọtini API fun ẹyìn Notion (ti a lo nipasẹ `genericBackend.ts` tito tito lẹyin ti a mọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Ipilẹ URL fun Notion API (le bori fun awọn ọna yiyan Notion ti ara ẹni ti gbalejo).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(a ko ṣeto) _              | bọtini API fun Obsidian Vault backend (lo nipasẹ `genericBackend.ts` tito tito lẹyin ti a mọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | URL mimọ fun Obsidian Vault API (le bori fun ifinkan latọna jijin).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | TV6 ti tẹ iranti ibajẹ oluwa yipada.** Jade (pa aiyipada)** - gbigba** npaarẹ** awọn iranti ti bajẹ. Pẹlu piparẹ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` jẹ telemetry mimọ ati pe ko si nkankan ti paarẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (awọn ọjọ) lẹhin eyi iranti `episodic` ti a ko lo yoo bajẹ. `0` ṣe ajẹsara episodic paapaa. Awọn oriṣi ti o tọ (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) jẹ ajesara nigbagbogbo. Aago ibajẹ tun-ipilẹ lori `last_accessed_at`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Iranti abẹrẹ `>=` yii ni ọpọlọpọ igba di ajesara si ibajẹ laibikita iru. `0` mu ajesara iwọle ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (alaabo)                | Àárín (ìṣẹ́jú àáyá) fún ìparun ìbàjẹ́ ìgbàkọọkan iyan ni `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/ unset = ko si gbigba igbakọọkan. Ijade-iwọle lẹmeji: tun nilo `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(a ko ṣeto) _              | Awọn ids olupese ti o ya sọtọ komama (aibikita ọran) ti o gba ifiranṣẹ `system` kan** nikan ni atọka 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Fun iwọnyi, kaṣe-ailewu aarin-array iranti splice jẹ ailewu ni awọn ibaraẹnisọrọ titan-pupọ, nitorinaa iranti jẹ merged/prepended bi ifiranṣẹ eto idari dipo. Awọn aiyipada si `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo` nikan; faagun fun ara ẹni ti gbalejo OpenAI-ibaramu awọn aaye ipari (fun apẹẹrẹ Qwen3.5 +/ 3.6) eyiti awoṣe iwiregbe nfi idinamọ-ifiranṣẹ eto-asiwaju kanṣoṣo. |

### Kekere-Ramu Docker Apeere

`128` jẹ dasibodu-nikan. Awọn aṣoju ifaminsi lori òkiti `FATAL ERROR` yii lakoko `/v1/responses` gigun. Maṣe lo apẹẹrẹ yii bi ẹnu-ọna Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`           | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Awọn orisun data ti o ya sọtọ-koma.     |

## Arena ELO Sync

| Ayípadà                      | Aiyipada            | Faili orisun                                                                         | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`              | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Igbakọọkan Arena AI leaderboard ELO amuṣiṣẹpọ, atunto lati Awọn asia Ẹya Dasibodu tabi pẹlu `false` lati jade.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`              | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Fọọmu ìpele ti a lo fun ids awoṣe ni `GET /v1/models`. `dual` n polowo mejeeji ìpele inagijẹ kukuru ati ìpele olupese canonical fun gbogbo awoṣe (ibaramu sẹhin — aijọju ilọpo meji katalogi); `alias` njade id kan fun awoṣe; `canonical` njade nikan ni kikun olupese-id ìpele (awọn olupese ti inagijẹ wọn tẹlẹ jẹ id canonical tọju titẹsi ẹyọkan wọn). Awọn alabara le bori fun ibeere pẹlu `?prefix=alias`. Wo [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (wakati 24) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Aago amuṣiṣẹpọ ni iṣẹju-aaya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

## PromptQL Olupese ibi isereile (Unofficial/Experimental)

Yipada-ẹrọ afara igba GraphQL fun kiakia.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Gbogbo iyan - awọn aseku tọka si awọn aaye ipari ibi-iṣere gbangba; idojuk nikan fun self-hosted/alternate PromptQL imuṣiṣẹ.

| Ayípadà                      | Aiyipada                                                           | Faili orisun                                                            | Apejuwe                                                                |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | GraphQL endpoint ti a lo fun awọn iṣẹ chat/session.                    |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | aaye ipari GraphQL ti a lo lati beere kirẹditi balance/usage.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Ipari ipari ti a lo fun isọdọtun-akitiyan tokini.                      |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Akoko ti o pọju (ms) lati didi `thread_events` ṣaaju ki akoko to jade. |

## Olupese Ayelujara HyperAgent (Unofficial/Experimental)

Afara igba ti a ṣe atunṣe-pada fun hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Iyan - awọn aaye aiyipada ni aaye ipari billing/usage ti gbogbo eniyan; idojuk nikan fun self-hosted/alternate HyperAgent imuṣiṣẹ.

| Ayípadà                | Aiyipada                                            | Faili orisun                            | Apejuwe                                                       |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `HYPERAGENT_USAGE_URL` | `https://hyperagent.com/api/settings/billing/usage` | `open-sse/services/usage/hyperagent.ts` | Endpoint ti a lo lati mu awọn bulọọki kirẹditi billing/usage. |
