## Kilo Code Lilo Quotas

Iwontunwonsi USD ti ara ẹni ati wiwa lilo Kilo Pass fun olupese koodu Kilo. Iyan - awọn aaye aiyipada ni gbangba Kilo API; idojuk nikan fun relay/test amuduro. Ijeri nlo ami iraye si OAuth ti o wa tẹlẹ.

| Ayípadà        | Aiyipada              | Faili orisun                          | Apejuwe                                                                          |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti a lo lati mu iwọntunwọnsi koodu Kilo ti ara ẹni ati lilo Kilo Pass. |

---

## Olupese wẹẹbu Adobe Firefly (Unofficial/Experimental)

Itura-itumọ ti ẹrọ aṣawakiri fun olupese wẹẹbu Adobe Firefly (`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Iyan - gbogbo awọn aseku wa ni aifwy fun fifi sori tabili deede.

> **Yiyọ kuro ni # 9255.** Akoko asiko asiko ti o somọ CDP atijọ ti Chrome (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts) ti rọpo nipasẹ iṣẹ buwolu aṣawakiri Playwright kan, ati pe awọn koko rẹ ko si mọ. Awọn oniyipada ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, pẹlu ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS ati ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, ko ka nibikibi ninu koodu koodu — ṣiṣeto wọn ko ni ipa.

| Ayípadà                              | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                                    | Apejuwe                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------ | -------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Ṣeto si `1` fun Chrome ti ko ni ori otitọ (ti a mọ-fọ fun ipilẹṣẹ; yokokoro nikan).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aṣàwákiri ìṣàfilọ́lẹ̀ tó gbóná opt-in/out. `0` jẹ́ kí gbóná tó máa ṣiṣẹ́ mọ́ (ìmúbọ̀sípò 408 àárín ìpele ṣi kan). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ṣeto si `0` lati mu idaduro igba Adobe Firefly si disk.                                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aafo ti o kere ju (ms) ti fi agbara mu laarin awọn ifisilẹ ti o tẹle, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Aafo afikun (ms) ti a ṣafikun lẹhin ipele aṣeyọri kan, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Idaduro ipilẹ (ms) ṣaaju fifisilẹ ibeere iran kan, ti o bori aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu.                         |

## 19. Awoṣe Amuṣiṣẹpọ (Dev)

| Ayípadà                             | Aiyipada            | Faili orisun                       | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(a ko ṣeto) _      | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Idojuk lile fun modeli.dev amuṣiṣẹpọ ifowoleri. Unset = ola Eto > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no`** bori DB** o si fo mejeeji amuṣiṣẹpọ igbakọọkan ati awọn ọlọjẹ `getModelsDevPricing()` SQL/JSON (imupadabọ nigbati dasibodu ti wa ni wiwọ lori iṣẹlẹ iṣẹlẹ kanna). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` awọn ologun amuṣiṣẹpọ lori. Ifowoleri save/clear ṣi pe `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, eyiti ko si-op labẹ isunmi iṣẹju 60 tabi `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (wakati 24) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Àkókò ìmúṣiṣẹ́pọ̀ àwòṣe àwòkọ́ṣe ìdàgbàsókè ní ìṣẹ́jú àáyá.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (wakati 24) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Aarin (awọn iṣẹju-aaya) fun atunṣe ti ara ẹni-atunṣe-window reconciler (5004): awọn olupese pinni-pipe awọn window lati wiwa `/models` bi `auto:discovery` ṣe bori nigbati wọn yapa lati inu iwe akọọlẹ naa. Ṣeto si `0` lati mu ṣiṣẹ. Tun lo data ti a muṣiṣẹpọ tẹlẹ (ko si bu tuntun); ko tun kọ `manual` danu.                                                                                                                                                          |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` jẹ nikan fun awọn relays ẹni-kẹta ti o gba awọn onibara koodu Claude ni iyasọtọ. OmniRoute atunkọ awọn ibeere ki awọn relays wọnyẹn gba wọn. Ti o ba fẹ nikan lo Claude Code CLI, tabi o ko ni idaniloju kini awọn isọdọtun wọnyi jẹ, tọju alaabo yii ki o ṣafikun olupese ibaramu Anthropic deede dipo.

---

| Ayípadà                                           | Aiyipada                 | Faili orisun                                                                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Ikuna-iyara ayẹwo ilera akoko ipari.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Abajade ayẹwo ilera TTL.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                    | Kaṣe TTL fun awọn iwadii ilera aṣoju ti kuna. Jeki eyi kuru ju `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` nitoribẹẹ awọn akoko isunmọ aṣoju tionkojalo labẹ atunda concurrency giga ni iyara laisi piparẹ-ikuna-yara fun awọn aṣoju ti o ku nitootọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Ṣeto `false` lati mu oluṣeto ilera aṣoju abẹlẹ kuro ti o ṣe iwadii awọn aṣoju ti o forukọsilẹ lorekore.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Àárí ìgbálé ìlera ìpilẹ̀ṣẹ̀ olùṣàkóso nínú ms ( `60000` tó kéré jù).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`               | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                       | Àárín àkókò ìwádìí nínú ms fún àwọn aṣojú-ojú-igbẹ́ (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), tí àwọn ìwádìí rẹ̀ jí àdádó aláìṣiṣẹ́mọ́ ( `60000` tó kéré jù).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                 | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                          | Aarin igbapada-kọja abẹlẹ ni ms: melo ni awọn aṣoju-aiṣedeede iṣaaju ti wa ni atunyẹwo nitoribẹẹ aṣoju ti o gba pada si yiyi laisi atunbere. Awọn iye ni isalẹ `60000` ṣubu pada si aiyipada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

### Ṣiṣan-imularada tuning ibakan

Ihuwasi idaduro imularada ti wa ni aifwy nipasẹ awọn alakọdi koodu ni `open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`), ti o han nibi fun itọkasi - yiyipada wọn nilo atunṣe koodu, kii ṣe env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — bawo ni ferese SSE šiši ti wa ni waye nitoribẹẹ truncation ni kutukutu ni a le tun gbiyanju ṣaaju ki eyikeyi baiti ṣe adehun si alabara.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` - fila lile lori window ti o waye; dá (fifọ + passthrough) ni kete ti yi ọpọlọpọ awọn baiti akojo, laiwo ti aago.
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` - max ṣiṣatunṣe ṣiṣafihan ṣiṣafihan ṣiṣan ti oke lakoko ti idaduro ṣi jẹ aifọwọsi.

> **Opin oṣuwọn sisun-window olupese (ko si env var):** FCC-ported fun-olupese sliding-window rate-limit _fallback_ wa ninu koodu (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, ti firanṣẹ nipasẹ `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) ṣugbọn awọn ọkọ oju omi pẹlu ẹya** ṣofo map aiyipada** o si ni** ko si oniṣẹ env var** loni - o ṣiṣẹ nikan nipasẹ kio idanwo / satunkọ koodu. O ti wa ni imomose ko akojọ si ni awọn tabili loke. Awọn per-`(token, IP)` relay limiter ti _does_ ni koko ni `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

## 22. N ṣatunṣe aṣiṣe

> [!CAUTION]
> Awọn oniyipada wọnyi gbejade** iṣẹjade ọrọ-ọrọ** ati ki o le jo kókó data.** Maṣe mu ṣiṣẹ ni iṣelọpọ.**

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada                        | Faili orisun                                                                                          | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(a ko ṣeto) _                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ṣeto `1` lati jẹ ki awọn iwe-igbimọ oluṣe verbose Cursor ṣiṣẹ (awọn chunks SSE ti a yipada, ati bẹbẹ lọ).                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(a ko ṣeto) _                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Inagijẹ ibaramu sẹhin ti `CURSOR_DEBUG`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(a ko ṣeto) _                  | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ona faili iyan ti o gba aise decoded Cursor chunks nigbati `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                        | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ṣiṣan akoko aiṣiṣẹ (ms) fun oluṣe Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Ṣeto `0` lati mu ṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                                           |

Tun lo bi ìpele itọsọna ti o yọ kuro ninu awọn bulọọki iṣaju eto iwoyi. Fi silẹ aito lati mu ṣiṣẹ.                        |

## 23. GitHub Integration

Gba awọn olumulo laaye lati jabo awọn ọran taara lati Dasibodu naa.

| Ayípadà               | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------- | -------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`     | Ibi ipamọ ni ọna kika `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`     | GitHub Àmi Wiwọle Ti ara ẹni pẹlu aaye `issues:write`.                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | oro triage / awọsanma oluranlowo oluranlọwọ | Jeneriki àmi iraye si GitHub ti a lo bi ipadasẹhin fun `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` ati jẹun nipasẹ awọn oluranlọwọ aṣoju awọsanma ni `src/lib/cloudAgent/*`. |

---

## Awọn oju iṣẹlẹ imuṣiṣẹ

Fun itọnisọna SRE backend (iwa ts/bifrost/auto, 9router vs CLIProxyAPI placement, and high-throughput fallback nwon.Mirza), wo [Relay Backend Strategy](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Ilọsiwaju Agbegbe ti o kere julọ

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Docker Production

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Afẹfẹ-gapped / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS pẹlu Aṣoju Yiyipada (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<generated>
API_KEY_SECRET=<generated>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<generated>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Apoti Iyanrin ogbon (v3.8.0 +)

Awọn ifilelẹ lọ ati awọn bọtini aabo ti a lo nigbati ilana Awọn ogbon (`src/lib/skills/`) nṣiṣẹ awọn adaṣe asọye olumulo ni agbegbe iyanrin.

| Ayípadà                           | Aiyipada                                      | Faili orisun                                                       | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (iṣẹju 10)                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Akoko akoko odi-ipaniyan fun awọn koodu oye apoti iyanrin. Fila lile; ohunkohun to gun ti wa ni pa.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(ṣubu pada si `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`) _ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Ipele-giga olorijori orchestration akoko ti pari. Ṣeto ti o ga ju `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` lati gba awọn ṣiṣan iṣẹ-igbesẹ lọpọlọpọ.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Awọn baiti ti o pọju ọgbọn le ka lati eyikeyi faili ti o ni apoti iyanrin.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                             | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Max awọn baiti sile lati eyikeyi nikan HTTP esi inu kan olorijori.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Fila lile lori awọn ohun kikọ stdout/stderr pada lati epe apoti iyanrin kan.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ṣeto `1`/`true` lati gba nẹtiwọki laaye lati inu apoti iyanrin. Awọn aiyipada si** ya sọtọ** fun aabo.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(sofo)_                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Akojọ iyọọda ti o ya sọtọ komama ti awọn aworan apoti idasilẹ fun ipaniyan apoti iyanrin. Ofo tumọ si aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu nikan.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(aiyipada ti a ṣe sinu)_                     | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Aworan apoti ti a lo nigbati o ba n tan apoti iyanrin ti o ṣe atilẹyin Docker. Daju lati pin aworan ipilẹ ti o ni lile ti aṣa.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                        | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Aago asiko apoti fun olorijori apoti: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` n yan akoko asiko ti o dara julọ ti a fi sori ẹrọ fun OS agbalejo (Apple Container/OrbStack lori macOS, apoti WSL lori Windows, Podman lori Linux), ja bo pada si Docker. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Muu `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` ṣiṣẹ ṣi ọna egress lati koodu ọgbọn lainidii. Papọ pẹlu `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` ati eto imulo `CORS_ORIGIN`/aṣoju ti o muna ni awọn imuṣiṣẹ pinpin.

## 25. Olupese Quotas, Tunnels, Backups & Misc Runtime

Awọn aaye ipari ipin awọn olupese, awọn tunnels nẹtiwọki (Tailscale, Ngrok, aṣoju aṣiṣe MITM ), adagun egress 1Proxy egress, awọn afẹyinti data ati awọn ẹya-ara kekere ti o dojukọ itọkasi nipasẹ Layer alaṣẹ tabi awọn iwe afọwọkọ.

| Ayípadà                                        | Aiyipada                                                                    | Faili orisun                                                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | okun asopọ Redis fun ẹhin opin opin oṣuwọn.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(alejo igbejade)_                                                          | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Daju agbalejo ti a lo lati gba Alibaba Bailian ifaminsi-eto awọn ipin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | yo lati ogun                                                                | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Ni kikun ipin URL idojuk fun Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                              | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Kuki igba console fun Qwen Cloud / Awoṣe Studio ti ara ẹni Tokini Ẹnu ọna ipin ipin (bọtini API itọkasi ko le ka). Daakọ gbogbo akọsori ibeere `Cookie` naa — o ni `login_qwencloud_ticket` ninu — lati eyikeyi ipe `api.json` si `cs-data.qwencloud.com` lori home.qwencloud.com › Idiyelé › Ṣiṣe alabapin (F12 › Nẹtiwọọki). Ifarabalẹ ati igba-dopin; fẹ aaye-asopọ `qwenCloudCookie` Dashboard.                                                                                                              |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                              | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Afọwọṣe `sec_token` idojuk fun ẹnu-ọna console Eto Token. Ni ifarabalẹ; nigbati unset awọn fetcher pinnu rẹ lati dashboard HTML lilo kukisi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Gbalejo Ẹnubodè idojuk fun Ẹkọ Ipinfunni ti ara ẹni Tokini (fun apẹẹrẹ `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` fun console Awoṣe Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Dasibodu URL ti a lo lati yanju `sec_token` lati inu HTML ti o wọle.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                              | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Kuki igba console fun Xiaomi MiMo Token Eto ipin API (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — bọtini API itọkasi ko le ka). Daakọ gbogbo akọsori ibeere `Cookie` - o ni `api-platform_serviceToken` ati `userId` — lati ibeere eyikeyi si `platform.xiaomimimo.com` lori oju-iwe Eto Tokini (F12 › Nẹtiwọọki). Ifarabalẹ ati igba-dopin; fẹ aaye-asopọ `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` Dashboard.                                                                                      |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Isuna oṣooṣu (Awọn kirẹditi) fun OmniRoute-opinpin Xiaomi MiMo fallback ti a lo nigbati ko si kuki console ni tunto. Awọn abawọn si Lite ipele 4.1B; ṣeto si awọn Kirediti ero (Standard 11B / Pro 38B / Max 82B). Asopọmọra-kọọkan `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` bori bori lori env var yii.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Console iwaju-opin ipa ọna idojuk fun mimu Alibaba awoṣe Studio ipin vision/media ipele-ọfẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Console iwaju-opin ipa ọna idojuk fun mimu Alibaba awoṣe Studio ipin-ọfẹ multimodal ipin.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Claude Warmup Scheduler

igbona-iwakọ Cron fun awọn asopọ Anthropic OAuth ti a yọ kuro, nitorinaa window iwọn-wakati 5 jẹ ṣiṣi nipasẹ ibeere ti a ṣeto ni kukuru dipo nipasẹ ọkan gidi akọkọ (#8848). Awọn iṣeto ni pipa ayafi ti `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` jẹ otitọ** ati** asopọ ti wa ni ifihan ni `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; atokọ asopọ ti o ṣofo tumọ si pe ko si ohun ti o gbona paapaa pẹlu env var lori.

| Ayípadà                        | Aiyipada                    | Faili orisun                 | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(kuro → kuro)_             | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Titunto si yipada fun oluṣeto igbona. Gba `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (aibikita ọran, gige). Eyikeyi iye miiran, tabi aito, fi oluṣeto silẹ.    |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ikosile cron aaye marun-un fun ami imorusi, ti a ṣe ayẹwo ni `America/Los_Angeles` (akoko aago atunto Anthropic) laibikita aago agbalejo. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Bawo ni ọpọlọpọ awọn asopọ ti wa ni warmed ni afiwe fun ami si. Dimole si `1`-`10`; iye ti kii ṣe oni-nọmba ṣubu pada si `3`.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Awoṣe ti a lo fun ibeere igbona. Paarẹ nikan ti aiyipada ko ba si lori ero rẹ; mu lawin awoṣe ti o ṣi awọn window.                        |

### Aṣàwákiri-Wọlé VNC Awọn akoko & Data-Dir Inagijẹ

Chromium +VNC ti a ṣe sinu ti a lo fun imudani ẹrọ aṣawakiri-iwọle ibaraenisepo (`/api/vnc-session`), pẹlu ogún `DATA_DIR` inagijẹ. Gbogbo iyan - awọn aipe VNC fojusi aworan `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` ti a dipọ ati pe o jẹ agbekọja nikan fun aworan eiyan aṣa, awọn ebute oko oju omi, tabi iṣatunṣe igbesi aye.

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada                                                                 | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | aami aworan Docker fun apoti iwọle Chromium +VNC. Kọ `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` tabi tọka eyi ni aworan aṣa.                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alakomeji asiko asiko apoti ti a lo lati ṣe ifilọlẹ apoti VNC (fun apẹẹrẹ ṣeto si `podman`).                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC/noVNC ibudo fara inu awọn eiyan.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ibudo inu awọn eiyan.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọna itọsọna profaili Chromium inu apo eiyan naa.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ilana igbasilẹ ti o duro awọn profaili aṣawakiri-iwọle.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (iṣẹju 10)                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago aiṣiṣẹ (ms) ṣaaju ki o to ikore igba VNC aiṣiṣẹ.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (30 min)                                                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Fila lile (ms) lori igba zqa VNC ni igbesi aye.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nọmba ti o pọju ti awọn akoko VNC nigbakanna.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago akoko (ms) nduro fun ẹrọ aṣawakiri ti a fi sinu apoti lati di CDP-ṣetan.                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago-akoko (ms) fun ikore session/cookies ti o gba lẹhin ti wiwọle ti pari.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Awọn asia laini aṣẹ afikun kọja si Chromium ti a fi sinu apoti.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nẹtiwọọki Docker ti a ṣe igbẹhin awọn apoti iwọle VNC darapọ mọ (#12571) dipo afara aiyipada, nitorinaa awọn apoti arakunrin ko le de ibudo afara CDP rẹ.                                        |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(a ko ṣeto) _                                                           | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Legacy inagijẹ** fun `DATA_DIR`, ṣayẹwo nikan lẹhin ti mejeeji `DATA_DIR` ati `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` ko ṣeto. O wa kaṣe igba o tẹle okun wẹẹbu Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Idanwo & E2E ijanu

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, ati `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Fi gbogbo iye silẹ ni isalẹ aito ni awọn imuṣiṣẹ iṣelọpọ.

| Ayípadà                                | Aiyipada                        | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | E2E bootstrap mode (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) fun Playwright olusare.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | ṣubu pada si `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọrọigbaniwọle abojuto itasi sinu agbegbe Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Pa idibo ayẹwo ilera agbegbe lakoko awọn ṣiṣe Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Muu OAuth tokini loop ṣayẹwo ilera lakoko awọn idanwo.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(a ko ṣeto) _                  | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Awọn olupese ti o ya sọtọ komama yọkuro kuro ninu gbigba-mimu isọdọtun ti nṣiṣẹ (fun apẹẹrẹ `codex,openai`). Omiiran ìfọkànsí si piparẹ ni kikun awọn olupese ilera — kukuru-TTL ntọju onitura lakoko ti awọn olupese kasikedi duro ni ifaseyin-nikan. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Pa ariwo ilera ṣayẹwo ni Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Rekọja iṣelọpọ Next.js ki o to bẹrẹ Playwright (iṣapejuwe CI ).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                             | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Rekọja kio aifi sipo OmniRoute (ti o nlo nipasẹ CI lati tọju `node_modules` mọ).                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                        | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Akoko duro (ms) fun olupin lati ni ilera ṣaaju ṣiṣe awọn idanwo ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`  | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL ẹfin ẹfin Electron nireti ohun elo ti a kojọpọ lati sin.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Lapapọ akoko ipari (ms) ṣaaju ki ijanu ẹfin to lọ silẹ.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto ferese (ms) lẹhin awọn ẹru oju-iwe naa.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(laifọwọyi)_                   | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ọna ti o han gbangba si ṣiṣiṣẹ Electron ti o ṣajọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Itọsọna data fun ṣiṣe ẹfin Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto `1` lati tọju ilana data ẹfin lẹhin ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ṣeto `1` lati san awọn akọọlẹ Electron si stdout lakoko ṣiṣe.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | # 7592: tun bẹrẹ lodi si dir data kanna ki o sọ ifilọlẹ keji yan awakọ SQLite abinibi.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(Ayẹwo PATH )_                 | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Yipada si ọna alakomeji Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

### Opopona onitumọ Docs

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (aṣẹ `npm run i18n:run` ). Gbogbo awọn oniyipada mẹfa ko ṣeto nipasẹ aiyipada — ṣeto wọn ni `.env` nikan lori awọn ẹrọ ti o yẹ ki o ni anfani lati ṣiṣẹ onitumọ docs.

| Ayípadà                                    | Aiyipada       | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------ | -------------- | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ṣii-ibaramu URL ipilẹ AI fun ẹhin itumọ.                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Àmi olùrùsókè fún ìpìlẹ̀ ìtumọ̀ (kò wọlé láé).                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Awoṣe id, fun apẹẹrẹ `gpt-4o-mini` tabi `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Ẹwọn awoṣe ti o ya sọtọ komama iyan gbiyanju ni ibere nigbati awoṣe akọkọ ba kuna (ipin 429, 401/402, 5xx, akoko ipari, nẹtiwọki); ibeere ti ko dara (400/413/422) ko tun gbiyanju ni ibomiiran.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`        | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Ibere-akoko akoko-ibere ni awọn iṣẹju-aaya.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Iṣakoso ironu iyan, firanṣẹ bi-si ẹhin nigba ti ṣeto.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`            | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Awọn ibeere itumọ ti o jọra nigbati o nṣiṣẹ lori ọpọlọpọ awọn faili / agbegbe.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` nlo ẹhin oke; `google` tumọ nipasẹ ẹrọ Google ti o ni aabo ati pe ko nilo ọkan ninu awọn oniyipada LLM.                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`            | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Pẹlu ẹrọ `llm`, chunk kan ti awoṣe ko le gbejade (oluṣọ ijade kuna lori gbogbo igbiyanju, tabi aṣiṣe awoṣe) jẹ itumọ nipasẹ Google dipo ikuna agbegbe naa. `0` mu u.                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Itumọ awọsanma Itumọ v2 bọtini. Unset nlo aaye ipari wẹẹbu ti ko ni bọtini (opin-opin, itanran fun awọn igbi lẹẹkọọkan).                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`            | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Awọn ibeere Google ti o jọra ti o pin nipasẹ gbogbo agbegbe ti ṣiṣe kan.                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`        | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Akoko akoko-ibere fun ẹnjini Google, ni milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                 |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(a ko ṣeto) _ | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Àmi kika-nikan fun ohun elo UI kit repo ti oke ikọkọ, ti o nlo nipasẹ `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` ati iṣiṣẹ iṣiṣẹ “UI Kit upstream watch” osẹ-sẹsẹ. Ni agbegbe ayẹwo naa nlo awọn iwe-ẹri git rẹ. |

## 27. Ifunni Radar (Alejo ti ara ẹni)

Iyan add-on gated nipasẹ awọn RADAR_ENABLED ẹya Flag (aiyipada pa — a ẹya-ara Flag toggled nipasẹ Settings/DB, ko env var; wo [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Awọn oniyipada mẹrin akọkọ ti o wa ni isalẹ jẹ awọn ifasilẹ iyan fun ifunni ti ara ẹni ti gbalejo tabi orita ati awọn ṣiṣan bọtini alatilẹyin. Karun, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, jẹ ọna asopọ aifọwọyi lọtọ lọtọ si igbimọ awọn iṣẹ ikọkọ ti eni. Wo [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) fun doc module ni kikun ati [imuṣiṣẹ ipari-si-opin ati ilana iṣeto-itọnisọna](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Oluka ikede Home/Changelog jeneriki ko ni tunto nipasẹ oniyipada ayika ati pe ko dale lori asia ẹya RADAR_ENABLED. O ka ibi ipamọ gbogbo eniyan `news.json` URL ti a kede ni `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` nipasẹ GET nikan; IDs yiyọ kuro ni ibi ipamọ agbegbe aṣawakiri.

| Ayípadà                       | Aiyipada                                     | Faili orisun                                                 | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti o pin nipasẹ katalogi ti a fowo si lọtọ, awọn itọkasi, awọn ifunni alatilẹyin, ati awọn ifunni Intel. Pari lati tọka si iṣẹ ti o gbalejo tabi orita. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(bọtini aiyipada ti a pin) _                | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ed25519 bọtini ita gbangba (base64-DER SPKI tabi PEM) ti a lo lati jẹrisi awọn ibuwọlu ifunni lati inu kikọ sii aṣa.                                              |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtini dasibodu "Mo jẹ oluranlọwọ" ṣi (ṣàn sisan bọtini alatilẹyin GitHub OAuth ).                                                                            |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL bọtini dasibodu "Ṣe atilẹyin iṣẹ akanṣe" ṣi (oju-iwe payment/plans ).                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(a ko ṣeto) _                               | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Olohun-nikan iṣiṣẹ ikọkọ-panel ọna asopọ. HTTPS nilo ayafi fun HTTP loopback SSH siwaju; aito tabi awọn iye aiṣedeede ko ṣẹda nkan lilọ kiri.                     |

## Ayẹwo: Awọn iyipada ti yọ kuro / ku

Awọn oniyipada atẹle han ni awọn ẹya ti tẹlẹ ti `.env.example` ṣugbọn wọn ni** ko si asiko isise to jo** ninu awọn ti isiyi codebase. Wọn ti yọ kuro:

| Ayípadà                                                                                                                                                                         | Idi                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Maṣe ka nipasẹ faili orisun eyikeyi. SQLite jẹ awakọ atilẹyin nikan - ko si yiyan ti o nilo.                                                       |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Wa ninu awọn awoṣe docs/env atijọ ṣugbọn a ko lo ni akoko asiko. Le pada ni ẹya-ara olona-apeere ojo iwaju.                                        |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Ko ṣe itọkasi ni koodu orisun. Iwọn aaye data ko ni opin lainidi.                                                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Ko ṣe itọkasi ni koodu orisun. O ṣeese ki afọmọ-julọ yọkuro.                                                                                       |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Ko forukọsilẹ ni `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                              |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Ko forukọsilẹ ni `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` ( Coding Kimi nlo OAuth, kii ṣe alakomeji CLI kan).                                           |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Ko ṣe itọkasi nibikibi ni koodu orisun.                                                                                                            |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. Akoko asiko ko ka awọn env vars wọnyi mọ — awọn iwe eri wa lati Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / encrypted DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. oluṣe Cursor `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (wo §22).                                                                |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ti yọ kuro ni v3.8.0. Kiro wa ni `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` - yiyi rẹ ko ni ipa.                                                            |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Yọ kuro lẹgbẹẹ olupese miiran ti ko lo awọn bọtini bọtini API ni v3.8.0.                                                                           |

### Aiyipada Iye Awọn atunṣe

| Ayípadà                   | Atijo `.env.example` Iye | Iyipada koodu gangan | Ti o wa titi                                        |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Kuro sinilona iye; `7` ti ṣe akọsilẹ bi aiyipada |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Kuro sinilona iye; `7` ti ṣe akọsilẹ bi aiyipada |

### atunto atunto OpenCode (ad-hoc tooling)

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` lati tun `opencode.json` kan ṣe pẹlu awọn iye `limit.context` deede ati awọn iye `limit.output` ti o fa lati apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute nṣiṣẹ. Ko si ọkan ninu iwọnyi ti a beere fun ṣiṣe deede - iwe afọwọkọ naa jẹ ohun elo oluṣe idagbasoke nikan.

| Ayípadà            | Aiyipada                 | Faili orisun                              | Apejuwe                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti apẹẹrẹ OmniRoute si ibeere fun `/v1/models`.                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(a ko ṣeto) _           | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | bọtini API lati jẹrisi lodi si aaye ipari `/v1/models` OmniRoute. Ṣubu pada si `OPENCODE_API_KEY` nigbati a ko ṣeto.          |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(a ko ṣeto) _           | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Kọ́kọ́rọ́ API ara koodu Ṣii (`sk-...`) ti a kọ sinu `opencode.json` ti a tun ṣe. Ṣubu pada si `OMNIROUTE_KEY` nigbati a ko ṣeto. |

### Funmorawon aisinipo-eval ijanu (irinṣẹ ad-hoc)

Ti a lo nipasẹ `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, igbelewọn funmorawon aisinipo CLI. Ko nilo fun iṣẹ ṣiṣe deede - ohun elo irinṣẹ idagbasoke nikan.

| Ayípadà                      | Aiyipada    | Faili orisun                        | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ----------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (sofo) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Awọn iwe eri JSON onišẹ ti pese fun olupese ti a ṣe adaṣe nipasẹ titẹkuro-eval CLI aisinipo (ti a ṣe itupalẹ pẹlu `JSON.parse`). Jẹ ki a ko ṣeto fun ṣiṣe gbigbẹ. |

### VNC Browser Sessions

Ti a lo nipasẹ `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` lati tunto awọn akoko Chromium alailori-orisun Docker fun awọn olupese ẹrọ aṣawakiri. Gbogbo iyan - awọn aseku han ni isalẹ.

| Ayípadà                               | Aiyipada                       | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ona si alakomeji Docker ti a lo lati spawn awọn apoti VNC.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | aworan Docker fun apoti Chromium VNC.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(awọn asia ti a ṣe sinu)_     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Afikun Chromium CLI args kọja si ẹrọ aṣawakiri inu apoti naa.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC ibudo inu awọn eiyan.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Chrome DevTools Protocol ibudo inu awọn eiyan.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ilana profaili inu eiyan naa.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Itọsọna ẹgbẹ-ogun fun awọn profaili aṣawakiri ti o tẹpẹlẹ.                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Àkókò tí kò ṣiṣẹ́ (ms) kí ìkórè VNC tó jẹ́ ìkórè.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Iye akoko ti o pọju (ms).                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Awọn akoko VNC nigbakanna ti o pọju.                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Aago imurasilẹ ti aṣawakiri (ms).                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | akoko asiko Harvest/cleanup (ms).                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Nẹtiwọọki Docker ti a ti sọtọ ti o darapọ mọ (# 12571), pa afara aiyipada. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Itọsọna fun itẹramọṣẹ o tẹle igba Notion.                                  |

### Ti abẹnu iṣẹ auth

| Ayípadà                                 | Aiyipada | Apejuwe                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –        | Àmi inline fun iṣakoso-ofurufu iṣẹ-si-iṣẹ ìfàṣẹsí.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –        | Ọna si faili ti o ni ami iṣẹ inu inu (ayanfẹ ninu awọn apoti; dojukọ oniyipada inline). |

| Ayípadà                             | Aiyipada  | Apejuwe                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Ṣeto si `false` lati foju gbigba OpenRouter fun awọn iṣiro olupese fun imudara katalogi. |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Globale Defaults für den headless Browser und den ausgehenden Ọpa-Tunnel. Im Dasibodu gesetzte Asopọ-Werte haben Vorrang.

| Ayípadà                                 | Aiyipada                       | Faili orisun                                                               | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Expliziter Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad für npm-, systemd-und PM2-Betrieb.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Gemeinsamer Fallback fun einen expliziten Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ti abẹnu CDP-Endpunkt; Docker verwendet den Sidecar auf Port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(kuro — kuro)_                | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Ṣeto si `1` (pẹlu atunto aaye ipari CDP kan) lati tun lo iṣakoso ita, profaili aṣawakiri ti a wọle si oju-iwe ChatGPT ti o wa dipo ipo ti o ya sọtọ fun titan; tun bo awọn taabu ChatGPT nigbakanna ni 1. Paa nipa aiyipada. Wo [profaili CDP o tẹsiwaju](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Wenn gesetzt, muss jede Anfrage an den CDP-Proxy-Sidecar diesen Wert im Header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` mitschicken (#13679). Ohne Wert leitet der Proxy unauthentifiziert weiter — dann schützt nur die Netzisolierung des Compose-Netzes `chatgpt-web-codex-net`. Erzeugen mit `openssl rand -hex 32`.  |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globale OpenAI-Tunnel-ID fun lilo Codex-Tool-Runden.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globaler Tunnel Runtime-Kọtini; niemals ni Logs ausgeben.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Exakter Name des neu erstellten ChatGPT-Aṣa-Connectors für die MCP-Brücke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Dediziertes Verzeichnis fun Browser-, Alagbata- ati Tunnelzustand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Jẹ `1` Browser-Diagnosebilder ati jedem Checkpoint erfasst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zu einem dauerhaften jiju-alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(Ṣawari-laifọwọyi)_           | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zum Bun-Runtime- Alakomeji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(a ko ṣeto) _                 | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Legacy-Fallback für `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; neue Setups verwenden den kanonischen Namen.                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

## OmniConductor Bridge

Onibara SSE ti o pẹ ti o ṣe afihan awọn iṣẹ-ṣiṣe ibudo OmniConductor sinu agbegbe A2A TaskManager (`src/lib/conductor/`). Jade- Afara nikan bẹrẹ nigbati `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL` ti ṣeto. Ẹgbẹ olupin nikan: ami ibudo ko gbọdọ de ẹrọ aṣawakiri naa rara.

| Ayípadà                        | Aiyipada                | Faili orisun                     | Apejuwe                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ipilẹ URL ti ibudo OmniConductor (fun apẹẹrẹ `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Unset = Afara alaabo.                         |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ijẹrisi ibudo fun ifunni SSE - gbejade `spokesperson` -kind ẹlẹgbẹ lori ibudo (`POST /v1/peers`, abojuto).           |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(sofo)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Ijẹrisi fun inbound A2A→ hub iṣẹ aṣoju iṣẹ (`POST /v1/tasks`); ṣubu pada si `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` nigbati a ko ṣeto. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | Ipilẹ URL ti iṣẹ agbẹnusọ (Faro) lẹhin aṣoju iwiregbe dasibodu (`/api/conductor/ask`).                               |

### Iṣeto-kiota-mọ

Ti a lo nipasẹ `open-sse/services/combo.ts` ati `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` fun awọn sọwedowo-isuna-ami-ibeere ṣaaju. Jade-iwa ipa ọna aiyipada ko yipada nigbati a ko ṣeto.

| Ayípadà                            | Aiyipada | Faili orisun                                | Apejuwe                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ---------------------------------- | -------- | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`      | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Nigba ti `1`, foo awọn asopọ ti isuna aami-window kọọkan (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tabili `provider_quota_state`) ko le ni idiyele idiyele ibeere ṣaaju fifiranṣẹ. Ikuna-ṣii nigbati ko si iṣeto isuna.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(lori)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Isọdiwọn esi ti iṣiro ami-iṣọ ọrọ-ọrọ (#14931): fun (olupese, awoṣe, awọn irinṣẹ ti o wa) EMA ti `usage.prompt_tokens` / iṣiro, dimọ si 0.15–2.0, ni iranti nikan. `off`, `0` tabi `false` mu gbigbasilẹ ati ohun elo ṣiṣẹ. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`  | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Ṣe iforukọsilẹ `xai-oauth` (`xao`) ati awọn ọna aworan `grok-cli`. Dasibodu ẹya asia ti kanna orukọ; yi danu DB bori lori iye yi.                                                                                           |
